const supabaseKey = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Inl0ZnBpeWZhcHZ5YmlobG5neGtzIiwicm9sZSI6ImFub24iLCJpYXQiOjE3NzAxMDA2MTIsImV4cCI6MjA4NTY3NjYxMn0.7TRVjvFjxVtxkP_hL3wnlxPT6FXJd0y7aKHNzDHThAQ'; const supabase = window.supabase.createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey); // CTO FEFO (First-Expiring, First-Out): chronological consumption by expiry, // NOT by credit application/posting date. Old deductions without an allocation // are reconstructed in FEFO order. New deductions pin their source IDs in remarks. const CTOFEFO = (() => { const EPS = 0.000001; const onlyDate = value => { const match = String(value ?? '').match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/); return match ? match[1] : ''; }; const today = () => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {timeZone:'Asia/Manila',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date()); const addYear = date => { if (!date) return ''; const d = new Date(date + 'T00:00:00Z'); if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; d.setUTCFullYear(d.getUTCFullYear()+1); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); }; const amount = row => Math.max(0, Math.abs(Number(row.calculated_hours ?? row.base_hours ?? 0) || 0)); const orderRef = row => { const value = String(row.PSO_Number ?? row.pso_number ?? '').trim(); return value ? (/^(PSO|RSO|SO)\b/i.test(value) ? value : 'PSO ' + value) : 'CTO credit #' + String(row.id ?? '?'); }; const isEarned = row => { const t=String(row.transaction_type||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const r=String(row.remarks||'').trim().toUpperCase(); return t==='EARNED' && !['APPLICATION','PENDING','REQUESTED','SUBMITTED','DISAPPROVED','REJECTED','CANCELLED'].includes(t) && !['FOR HR PROCESSING','ENCODED','PENDING','APPLICATION','FOR PROCESSING'].includes(r) && !/^(DISAPPROVED|REJECTED|CANCELLED)/.test(r); }; const isUsed = row => { const t=String(row.transaction_type||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const r=String(row.remarks||'').trim().toUpperCase(); return t==='USED'||t==='CTO DEDUCTION'||r==='CTO DEDUCTION'||r.startsWith('CTO DEDUCTION |'); }; const expiry = row => onlyDate(row.expires_on) || addYear(onlyDate(row.pso_date||row.date||row.created_at)); const earnedDate = row => onlyDate(row.pso_date||row.date||row.created_at||row.posted_at); const useDate = row => onlyDate(row.leave_date||row.date||row.posted_at||row.created_at); const compareBuckets = (a,b) => (a.expiresOn||'9999-12-31').localeCompare(b.expiresOn||'9999-12-31') || a.earnedDate.localeCompare(b.earnedDate) || String(a.record.id??'').localeCompare(String(b.record.id??''),undefined,{numeric:true}); // Each stored new USED transaction has an audit tag, e.g. FEFO:72=3.000,76=5.000. // BASE is used only for the pre-system opening balance without an expiry date. const readPinned = remarks => { const m=String(remarks||'').match(/(?:^|\s|\|)FEFO:([A-Za-z0-9_=.\-,]+)/); if(!m) return null; return m[1].split(',').map(part=>{ const i=part.lastIndexOf('='); return i<1?null:{id:part.slice(0,i),hours:Math.max(0,Number(part.slice(i+1))||0)}; }).filter(Boolean); }; const snapshot = (employee, allRecords, asOf=today()) => { const cutoff=onlyDate(asOf)||today(); const empId=String(employee?.emp_id ?? ''); const records=(allRecords||[]).filter(r=>!empId || String(r.emp_id)===empId); const buckets=records.filter(isEarned).map(record=>({ record, id:String(record.id ?? ''), ref:orderRef(record), earnedDate:earnedDate(record), expiresOn:expiry(record), originalHours:amount(record), remainingHours:amount(record) })).filter(b=>b.earnedDate && b.earnedDate<=cutoff).sort(compareBuckets); let openingRemaining=Math.max(0,Number(employee?.cto_balance||0)||0); let unmatchedHours=0; const uses=records.filter(isUsed).filter(r=>{const d=useDate(r);return !!d && d<=cutoff;}) .sort((a,b)=>useDate(a).localeCompare(useDate(b)) || Number(a.id||0)-Number(b.id||0)); for(const use of uses){ const date=useDate(use); let required=amount(use); const pinned=readPinned(use.remarks); if(pinned){ for(const item of pinned){ if(required<=EPS)break; const qty=Math.min(required,item.hours); if(item.id==='BASE'){ const take=Math.min(openingRemaining,qty);openingRemaining-=take;unmatchedHours+=qty-take; }else{ const bucket=buckets.find(b=>b.id===item.id); const take=bucket?Math.min(bucket.remainingHours,qty):0; if(bucket)bucket.remainingHours-=take; unmatchedHours+=qty-take; } required-=qty; } // Any unexpected unmatched amount is not silently assigned to another PSO. unmatchedHours+=Math.max(0,required); }else{ for(const bucket of buckets){ if(required<=EPS)break; if(bucket.earnedDate>date || (bucket.expiresOn && date>=bucket.expiresOn))continue; const take=Math.min(required,bucket.remainingHours); bucket.remainingHours-=take;required-=take; } if(required>EPS){const take=Math.min(required,openingRemaining);openingRemaining-=take;required-=take;} unmatchedHours+=Math.max(0,required); } } const active=buckets.filter(b=>b.earnedDate<=cutoff && (!b.expiresOn||cutoff<b.expiresOn) && b.remainingHours>EPS); const availableHours=openingRemaining+active.reduce((sum,b)=>sum+b.remainingHours,0); const expiredHours=buckets.filter(b=>b.expiresOn && cutoff>=b.expiresOn) .reduce((sum,b)=>sum+b.remainingHours,0); const plan=(requested, forDate=cutoff) => { let needed=Math.max(0,Number(requested)||0); const selections=[]; for(const b of active){ if(needed<=EPS)break; if(b.earnedDate>forDate || (b.expiresOn&&forDate>=b.expiresOn))continue; const hrs=Math.min(needed,b.remainingHours); if(hrs>EPS){selections.push({id:b.id,ref:b.ref,hours:hrs,expiresOn:b.expiresOn});needed-=hrs;} } if(needed>EPS && openingRemaining>EPS){const hrs=Math.min(needed,openingRemaining); selections.push({id:'BASE',ref:'Opening CTO balance',hours:hrs,expiresOn:''});needed-=hrs;} return {selections,unfundedHours:Math.max(0,needed),hoursUsed:Math.max(0,Number(requested)||0)-Math.max(0,needed)}; }; return {availableHours,openingRemaining,expiredHours,unmatchedHours,buckets,active,nextToExpire:active[0]||null,plan}; }; const tag = plan => 'FEFO:'+plan.selections.map(b=>`${b.id}=${b.hours.toFixed(3)}`).join(','); const label = plan => plan.selections.map(b=>`${b.ref}: ${b.hours.toFixed(3)}h${b.expiresOn?' (expires '+b.expiresOn+')':''}`).join('; '); return {snapshot,today,expiry,earnedDate,orderRef,isEarned,isUsed,tag,label,onlyDate,addYear}; })(); // GLOBAL STATE VARIABLES let currentUser = null; let itineraryDraft = []; }; // PASSWORD HASHING UTILITY window.hashPassword = async function(msg) { if (!msg) return ""; return sha256(msg); }; // WEBAUTHN UTILS const bufferToBase64 = (buf) => btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(buf))); // Earth's radius in meters const p1 = lat1 * Math.PI / 180; const p2 = lat2 * Math.PI / 180; const dp = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180; const dl = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180; const a = Math.sin(dp / 2) * Math.sin(dp / 2) + Math.cos(p1) * Math.cos(p2) * Math.sin(dl / 2) * Math.sin(dl / 2); return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a)); }; // UNDERTIME CALCULATION HELPER const calcUndertime = (timeLogs, schedule, schedType) => { if (!schedule || timeLogs.length === 0) return { h: 0, m: 0 }; let amIn = null, amOut = null, pmIn = null, pmOut = null; if(timeLogs.length > 0) amIn = timeLogs[0]; if(timeLogs.length > 1) amOut = timeLogs[1]; if(timeLogs.length > 2) pmIn = timeLogs[2]; if(timeLogs.length > 3) pmOut = timeLogs[3]; const endMins = parseInt(schedEndParts[0]) * 60 + parseInt(schedEndParts[1]); const lunchStart = 12 * 60; // 720 const lunchEnd = 13 * 60; // 780 if (in1 !== null && in1 > startMins) totalUndertimeMinutes += (in1 - startMins); if (out1 !== null && out1 < lunchStart) totalUndertimeMinutes += (lunchStart - out1); if (in2 !== null && in2 > lunchEnd) totalUndertimeMinutes += (in2 - lunchEnd); if (out1 !== null && in2 !== null && (in2 - out1) < 1) { totalUndertimeMinutes += 1 - (in2 - out1); } if (out2 !== null && out2 < endMins) totalUndertimeMinutes += (endMins - out2); } else { let workMins = 0; const calcDur = (start, end) => (start === null || end === null) ? 0 : end - start; let amDur = calcDur(in1, out1 || (out2 && out2 < 780 ? out2 : 720)); let pmDur = calcDur(in2 || (in1 && in1 > 780 ? in1 : 780), out2); if (in1 !== null && out2 !== null && out1 === null && in2 === null) { let diff = out2 - in1; if (in1 < 720 && out2 > 780) diff -= 60; workMins = diff; } else { workMins = amDur + pmDur; } if (out1 !== null && in2 !== null && (in2 - out1) < 1) { workMins -= (1 - (in2 - out1)); } if (workMins > 0 && workMins < 480) { totalUndertimeMinutes = 480 - workMins; curY += 5; }); window.closeTevModal(); await window.saveSecurePDF(doc, `TEV_Claim_${currentTevToNo}_Daily.pdf`); } catch(e) { alert("PDF Gen Error: " + e.message); } }; // LEAVE LEDGER PARITY: identical routine used by both HR and employee portals. // Opening balances in employees are baselines, never the computed closing balances. const LeaveParity = (() => { const up = v => String(v ?? '').trim().toUpperCase(); const num = v => Number.isFinite(Number(v)) ? Math.abs(Number(v)) : 0; const todayYear = () => Number(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Manila',year:'numeric'}).format(new Date())); const category = value => { const s=up(value); if (/ELECTION SERVICE CREDIT|^ESCL?$/.test(s)) return 'ESC'; if (/COMPENSATORY|^CTO\b|CREDIT TO CTO/.test(s)) return 'CTO'; if (/SPECIAL PRIVILEGE|\bSPL\b|PREVELEGE/.test(s)) return 'SPL'; if (/WELLNESS|\bWL\b/.test(s)) return 'WL'; if (/SICK|\bSL\b/.test(s)) return 'SL'; if (/VACATION|MANDATORY|FORCED|FORCE LEAVE|\bVL\b|GATE.?PASS|UNDERTIME|TARDINESS/.test(s)) return 'VL'; return ''; }; const annualAllowance = (employee, field, cap, year) => { // Opening balance is only a current-year baseline. Next calendar year // receives its own fresh allowance: never carry over unused days. if (Number(year) !== todayYear()) return cap; const rawOpening=employee?.[field]; const opening=(rawOpening===null||rawOpening===undefined||rawOpening==='') ? NaN : Number(rawOpening); return Number.isFinite(opening) ? Math.min(cap, Math.max(0, opening)) : cap; }; const balance = (employee, rows, year=todayYear()) => { const result={ VL:Number(employee?.vl_balance||0), SL:Number(employee?.sl_balance||0), SPL:annualAllowance(employee,'spl_balance',3,year), WL:annualAllowance(employee,'wl_balance',5,year), ESC:Number(employee?.election_service_credit_balance||0) }; for (const row of rows||[]) { if (String(row.emp_id)!==String(employee?.emp_id)) continue; const tx=up(row.transaction_type), remarks=up(row.remarks); // Applications, reversals, denials and historical tracking do not debit balances. if (!['APPROVED','CREDIT','DEDUCTION','POSTED','MANUAL INPUT','FORFEITURE','GATE_PASS_DEDUCTION'].includes(tx)) continue; if (tx==='MANUAL INPUT' && remarks.includes('HISTORICAL_MANDATORY_TRACKING')) continue; // The original APPROVED debit is already removed or reduced on retraction. if (tx==='CREDIT' && /RETRACT|REFUND|REVERS|RESTOR|CANCEL/.test(remarks)) continue; const cat=category(row.type); if (!cat || cat==='CTO') continue; const rowYear=Number(String(row.start_date||row.created_at||'').slice(0,4)); if ((cat==='SPL'||cat==='WL') && rowYear!==Number(year)) continue; let debit=num(row.debit); if (cat==='VL' && /CREDIT(?:ED)? TO CTO/.test(remarks)) { const match=remarks.match(/VL CHARGE:\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/); debit=match?num(match[1]):0; } result[cat]+=num(row.credit)-debit; } // Annual SPL and WL can never exceed 3 and 5 respectively, even if a // legacy manual credit exists in the ledger. No inter-year carryover. result.SPL=Math.max(0,Math.min(3,result.SPL)); result.WL=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,result.WL)); return result; }; const trackType = row => { const kind=up(row.type),tx=up(row.transaction_type),remarks=up(row.remarks); if (!/VACATION|MANDATORY|FORCED|FORCE LEAVE|\bVL\b/.test(kind)) return ''; if (/GATE PASS|UNDERTIME|CHARGED FROM|EARNED|FORFEITURE/.test(kind)) return ''; if (/CANCELLED|CANCELED|RETRACTED|WITHDRAWN|FORFEITURE|DEDUCTION|CREDIT/.test(tx)) return ''; if (tx==='MANUAL INPUT') return remarks.includes('HISTORICAL_MANDATORY_TRACKING')?'HISTORICAL':''; if (/DISAPPROV|REJECT|DENIED/.test(tx)||/ROUTING_STATUS:\s*(DISAPPROV|REJECT|DENIED)/.test(remarks)) return 'DISAPPROVED'; if (tx==='APPROVED') return 'APPROVED'; if (/APPLICATION|PENDING|REQUESTED|SUBMITTED/.test(tx))return 'APPLICATION'; return ''; }; const mandatory = (rows, year=todayYear(), employeeId=null) => { const ids=new Set();let hours=0,deniedHours=0; for(const r of rows||[]){ if(employeeId!==null && String(r.emp_id)!==String(employeeId)) continue; if(Number(String(r.start_date||'').slice(0,4))!==Number(year))continue; if(ids.has(String(r.id)))continue; ids.add(String(r.id)); const kind=trackType(r);if(!kind)continue; const h=kind==='HISTORICAL' ? Number((String(r.remarks||'').match(/HISTORICAL_HOURS:\s*([0-9.]+)/i)||[])[1]||0) : num(r.debit)*8; if(!Number.isFinite(h)||h<=0)continue; hours+=h; if(kind==='DISAPPROVED')deniedHours+=h; } return {requiredHours:40,recordedHours:Math.min(40,hours),totalHours:hours,deniedHours,remainingHours:Math.max(0,40-hours),remainingDays:Math.max(0,40-hours)/8}; }; const eligibleStatus = row => { const tx=up(row.transaction_type); return /^(APPLICATION|APPROVED|DISAPPROVED|CANCELLED|CANCELED|RETRACTED|REQUESTED|SUBMITTED|PENDING(?:[_ ]|$))/.test(tx); }; // Stable pages prevent the default Supabase 1000-record cap from silently losing old entries. const fetchAll = async (client,queryFactory,pageSize=1000) => { const result=[]; for(let start=0;start<100000;start+=pageSize){ const {data,error}=await queryFactory(client).range(start,start+pageSize-1); if(error)throw error; result.push(...(data||[])); if(!data||data.length<pageSize)break; } return result; }; return {balance,mandatory,trackType,eligibleStatus,fetchAll,category,todayYear}; })(); window.loadCredits = async function() { const container = document.getElementById('leave-balances-container'); const ctoContainer = document.getElementById('cto-posting-container'); container.innerHTML = 'Loading...'; ctoContainer.innerHTML = 'Loading...'; // 1. Continue?`)) { if (!stale()) { container.innerHTML = ''; itineraryDraft = []; conflictsToProcess = []; } return; } } const conflictNos = [...new Set(conflictsByDate.get(dateStr) || [])]; for (const toNo of conflictNos) { const to = toByNo.get(toNo); if (!to) continue; const status = String(to.status || '').toUpperCase(); if (status.includes('DISAPPROVED')) continue; const promptMsg = (status === 'APPROVED' || status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT') ? `Conflict: Approved TO ${to.to_no} exists on ${dateStr}. Overwrite? (This will trigger SPLIT logic for multi-day TOs)` : `Conflict: Pending TO ${to.to_no} exists on ${dateStr}. Overwrite?`; if (confirm(promptMsg)) { localConflicts.push({ to_no: to.to_no, status: to.status, affected_date: dateStr }); } else { if (!stale()) { container.innerHTML = ''; itineraryDraft = []; conflictsToProcess = []; } return; } } // Generation-specific IDs prevent a stale request from sharing field IDs. const suffix = `${currentGenId}-${i}`; const cardId = `card-${suffix}`; const destId = `dest-${suffix}`; const purpId = `purp-${suffix}`; const fundId = `fund-${suffix}`; const repId = `rep-${suffix}`; const unitId = isPBDD ? `unit-${suffix}` : null; let selectedOptions = pbddOptions; if (isPBDD) { const targetUnit = prev.unit === 'NA' ? '' : String(prev.unit || ''); if (targetUnit === '') { selectedOptions = selectedOptions.replace('<option value="">', '<option value="" selected>'); } else { selectedOptions = selectedOptions.replace(`value="${targetUnit}"`, `value="${targetUnit}" selected`); } } const pbddSelect = isPBDD ? ` <select id="${unitId}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;"> ${selectedOptions} </select>` : ''; const previousDraft = localDraft.length ? localDraft[localDraft.length - 1] : null; const copyPrevious = previousDraft ? `<label style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; font-size:11px; font-weight:normal; margin-bottom:8px; color:var(--text-muted); cursor:pointer; white-space:nowrap;"> <input type="checkbox" style="width:auto; margin:0; padding:0;" onchange="if(this.checked) document.getElementById('${purpId}').value = document.getElementById('${previousDraft.purpId}').value; else document.getElementById('${purpId}').value = '';"> Copy previous purpose </label>` : ''; htmlParts.push(` <div class="card" id="${cardId}" style="border-left:4px solid var(--dar-green);"> <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:8px;"> <h5 style="margin:0; color:var(--dar-green); font-size:13px;">📅 ${dateStr} ${dayHasPSO ? '<span style="color:red; font-size:9px;">(HAS PSO)</span>' : ''}</h5> <button onclick="window.removeItinCard('${cardId}', '${dateStr}')" style="background:none; border:none; color:red; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;">DELETE</button> </div> <input type="text" id="${destId}" placeholder="Destination" value="${escAttr(prev.dest)}"> ${copyPrevious} <textarea id="${purpId}" rows="2" placeholder="Purpose">${escText(prev.purp)}</textarea> <input type="text" id="${fundId}" list="fund-sources-list" placeholder="Fund Source (Select or Type)" value="${escAttr(prev.fund)}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;"> ${pbddSelect} <input type="text" id="${repId}" placeholder="Report To" value="${escAttr(prev.rep)}"> </div>`); localDraft.push({ date: dateStr, destId, purpId, repId, fundId, unitId, cardId, hasPSO: dayHasPSO }); } if (stale()) return; // The ONLY commit for this generation. container.innerHTML = htmlParts.join(''); itineraryDraft = localDraft; conflictsToProcess = localConflicts; } catch (e) { if (currentGenId !== itinGenCounter) return; container.innerHTML = ''; itineraryDraft = []; conflictsToProcess = []; alert('Unable to build itinerary: ' + (e.message || e)); } }; window.submitTO = async function() { if (isSubmitting) return; if (itineraryDraft.length === 0) return alert("Itinerary is empty."); const uniqueItineraryDraft = []; const seenSubmitDates = new Set(); itineraryDraft.forEach(it => { if (!seenSubmitDates.has(it.date)) { seenSubmitDates.add(it.date); uniqueItineraryDraft.push(it); } }); itineraryDraft = uniqueItineraryDraft; const batchItin = []; const reportTos = []; const pbddUnits = new Set(); const fundSources = new Set(); for (let it of itineraryDraft) { const destEl = document.getElementById(it.destId); const purpEl = document.getElementById(it.purpId); const repEl = document.getElementById(it.repId); const fundEl = document.getElementById(it.fundId); const unitEl = it.unitId ? document.getElementById(it.unitId) : null; // Never silently skip a date: stop instead of submitting an incomplete itinerary. if (!destEl || !purpEl || !repEl) { return alert(`Itinerary for ${it.date} is out of sync. Please regenerate the date range before submitting.`); } const dest = destEl.value.trim(); const purp = purpEl.value.trim(); const rep = repEl.value.trim(); const fund = fundEl ? fundEl.value.trim() : ''; if (!dest || !purp || !rep) return alert(`Fill all fields (except Fund Source) for ${it.date}.`); batchItin.push({ travel_date: it.date, destination: dest, purpose: purp }); // Preserve the report-to person/office for its actual travel date. // Use the existing travel_orders.report_to column; no database migration. reportTos.push({date:it.date, value:rep}); if (fund) fundSources.add(fund); // Only add to array if not blank // Safely capture selected units if (unitEl) { let uVal = unitEl.value.trim(); if (uVal) pbddUnits.add(uVal); } } isSubmitting = true; const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-to'); if(submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = true; submitBtn.innerText = "Saving itinerary..."; } try { // Fetch full details of all personnel draft to route correctly per their distinct unit and division const empIds = personnelDraft.map(p => p.emp_id); const { data: empDetails, error: empDetailsError } = await supabase.from('employees') .select('emp_id, division, unit, position').in('emp_id', empIds); if (empDetailsError) throw empDetailsError; let finalDiv = currentUser.division; let finalUnit = currentUser.unit; let isPBDD = String(finalDiv).toUpperCase().includes('PBDD'); let isOtherDivision = false; let toNoDiv = currentUser.division ? currentUser.division.split(',')[0].trim() : "GEN"; if (isPBDD) { let units = new Set(); let hasValidUnitSelected = false; // Step 1: Add whatever cross-unit they selected in the dropdowns pbddUnits.forEach(u => { if (u !== '') { units.add(u); hasValidUnitSelected = true; } }); // Step 2: If they selected a cross-unit (like EDES), force their ORIGINAL unit (like SIB/SMEDSS) // to join the string so BOTH Unit Heads must approve. if (hasValidUnitSelected && currentUser.unit && currentUser.unit !== 'NA') { units.add(currentUser.unit); } // This will output something like "SIB/SMEDSS, EDES" or "" (Blank) if NA was chosen. finalUnit = Array.from(units).join(', '); } if (String(currentUser.s_assignment).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE') { const checkedBoxes = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="special_div"]:checked'); if(checkedBoxes.length > 0) { const selectedDivs = Array.from(checkedBoxes).map(cb => cb.value.trim()); const primaryDiv = toNoDiv; if (!selectedDivs.includes(primaryDiv) && selectedDivs.length > 0) { isOtherDivision = true; toNoDiv = selectedDivs[0]; finalDiv = primaryDiv + ", " + selectedDivs.join(', '); } else { finalDiv = selectedDivs.join(', '); } } } // Do not delete original data here; keep until safe submission for (let conflict of conflictsToProcess) { if (conflict.status.includes("PENDING")) { const { data: others } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('emp_id').eq('to_no', conflict.to_no).neq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id); await supabase.from('travel_orders').delete().eq('to_no', conflict.to_no).eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id); if (!others || others.length === 0) { await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', conflict.to_no); } await window.logActivity(conflict.to_no, 'WITHDRAWN_CONFLICT', `User ${currentUser.full_name} withdrew due to conflict override`); } else if (conflict.status === "APPROVED" || conflict.status === "READY_FOR_PRINT") { const { data: oldItin } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('*').eq('to_no', conflict.to_no).order('travel_date'); const remainingDays = oldItin.filter(day => day.travel_date !== conflict.affected_date); await supabase.from('travel_orders').update({ status: 'CANCELLED' }).eq('to_no', conflict.to_no); await window.logActivity(conflict.to_no, 'SPLIT_CANCELLED', `Approved TO split/overwritten on ${conflict.affected_date}`); await supabase.from('notifications').insert({ emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, message: `Approved TO ${conflict.to_no} was split/overwritten by ${currentUser.full_name}. Affected date: ${conflict.affected_date}`, sender: currentUser.full_name }); if (remainingDays.length > 0) { const remainderToNo = await window.generateToNo(currentUser.division ? currentUser.division.split(',')[0].trim() : "GEN"); const { data: origTO } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', conflict.to_no); const batchRemainder = origTO.map(({ id, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, to_no: remainderToNo, status: rest.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' ? 'READY_FOR_PRINT' : 'APPROVED' })); const batchRemainderItin = remainingDays.map(({ id, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, to_no: remainderToNo })); const { error: remErr1 } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').insert(batchRemainder); if (remErr1) throw new Error("Conflict TO Insert Error: " + remErr1.message); const { error: remErr2 } = await supabase.from('itinerary').insert(batchRemainderItin); if (remErr2) throw new Error("Conflict Itinerary Insert Error: " + remErr2.message); await window.logActivity(remainderToNo, 'SPLIT_CREATED', `Created from split of ${conflict.to_no}`); } } } const finalToNo = editingToNo ? editingToNo : await window.generateToNo(toNoDiv); const transport = document.getElementById('to-transport').value; const isGovVeh = transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE"; // <-- Ito yung nawala kaya nag-error! // Formats standard choices, types custom entries as OTHERS, or returns a clean blank value if empty let fundSourceArray = Array.from(fundSources).map(f => { if (!f || f.trim() === "") return ""; const up = f.trim().toUpperCase(); return ["GAS", "LTSP", "AARC", "ALI/PALA", "ARBDSP"].includes(up) ? up : `OTHERS: ${f.trim()}`; }).filter(f => f !== ""); let fundSource = fundSourceArray.length > 0 ? fundSourceArray.join(', ') : ""; let baseRemarks = []; if (itineraryDraft.some(it => it.hasPSO)) baseRemarks.push("EXISTING PSO"); if (document.getElementById('to-outside-bicol').checked) baseRemarks.push("OUTSIDE BICOL"); if (isGovVeh) { const depTime = document.getElementById('to-dep-time').value; if (depTime) { let [h, m] = depTime.split(':'); let ampm = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM'; let h12 = h % 12 || 12; baseRemarks.push(`Departure: ${h12}:${m} ${ampm}`); } } // Map specific statuses dynamically to ALL listed personnel individually const batchPersonnel = personnelDraft.map(p => { let empRecord = empDetails?.find(e => e.emp_id === p.emp_id) || p; let pUnit = empRecord.unit ? empRecord.unit.trim() : ""; let pDiv = empRecord.division ? empRecord.division.trim() : finalDiv; let pPos = empRecord.position ? String(empRecord.position).toUpperCase() : ""; // For the author making the request, assign the dynamically bonded units if (p.emp_id === currentUser.emp_id) { pUnit = finalUnit ? String(finalUnit) : ""; pDiv = finalDiv ? String(finalDiv) : ""; } if (pUnit === "NA") pUnit = ""; // Itineraries are immediately printable; no online approval or e-signature routing. // Unit, division, role, and signatory information remain preserved. const pRouteStatus = "READY_FOR_PRINT"; return { to_no: finalToNo, emp_id: p.emp_id, emp_name: p.name, unit: pUnit, division: pDiv, status: pRouteStatus, transport, fund_source: fundSource, report_to: reportTos.map(r => r.date + ' - ' + r.value).join(' | '), remarks: baseRemarks.join(" | ") }; }); batchItin.forEach(it => it.to_no = finalToNo); if (editingToNo) { await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', editingToNo); await supabase.from('travel_orders').delete().eq('to_no', editingToNo); } const { error: toError } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').insert(batchPersonnel); if (toError) throw new Error("Travel Order DB Error: " + toError.message); const { error: itinError } = await supabase.from('itinerary').insert(batchItin); if (itinError) throw new Error("Itinerary DB Error: " + itinError.message); await window.logActivity(finalToNo, 'READY_FOR_PRINT', 'Itinerary created; available for printing without digital approval or e-signature'); alert("Itinerary saved and ready for printing: " + finalToNo); document.getElementById('itin-from').value = ''; document.getElementById('itin-to').value = ''; document.getElementById('dynamic-itin-fields').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('to-transport').selectedIndex = 0; document.getElementById('to-outside-bicol').checked = false; document.getElementById('to-dep-time').value = ''; document.getElementById('dep-time-group').style.display = 'none'; personnelDraft = [{ emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, name: currentUser.full_name, unit: currentUser.unit }]; window.renderPersonnel(); itineraryDraft = []; conflictsToProcess = []; editingToNo = null; window.switchTab('status-view', document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item')[3]); } catch(e) { alert("Error: " + e.message); } finally { isSubmitting = false; if(submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = false; submitBtn.innerText = "💾 SAVE ITINERARY — READY TO PRINT"; } } }; window.generateToNo = async function(targetDiv) { const year = new Date().getFullYear(); const div = (targetDiv || currentUser.division || "GEN").split(',')[0].trim().substring(0, 5).toUpperCase(); const isSplit = currentUser && String(currentUser.type).toUpperCase() === 'SPLIT'; // AJDP division is a single continuous annual sequence, regardless of SPLIT type. // Include existing AJDP-SPLIT references to avoid restarting or duplicating numbers. if (isAJDPDivision(targetDiv || currentUser?.division)) { const ajdpYear = todayStr.slice(0, 4); let maxSeq = 0; let offset = 0; const pageSize = 500; while (true) { const { data: existing, error } = await supabase.from('travel_orders') .select('to_no').like('to_no', `${ajdpYear}-AJDP-%`) .order('to_no', { ascending: true }) .range(offset, offset + pageSize - 1); if (error) throw error; for (const row of existing || []) { const match = String(row.to_no || '').match(/^\d{4}-AJDP-(?:SPLIT-)?(\d+)$/i); if (match) maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, Number(match[1])); } if (!existing || existing.length < pageSize) break; offset += pageSize; } return `${ajdpYear}-AJDP-${String(maxSeq + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`; } let seq = 1; if (isSplit) { const { data } = await supabase.from('travel_orders') .select('to_no') .like('to_no', `${year}-%-SPLIT-%`); if (data && data.length > 0) { let maxSeq = 0; data.forEach(row => { const parts = row.to_no.split('-'); const parsed = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10); if (!isNaN(parsed) && parsed > maxSeq) { maxSeq = parsed; } }); seq = maxSeq + 1; } return `${year}-${div}-SPLIT-${String(seq).padStart(5, '0')}`; } else { const { data } = await supabase.from('travel_orders') .select('to_no') .like('to_no', `${year}-${div}-%`) .not('to_no', 'like', '%-SPLIT-%') .order('to_no', { ascending: false }) .limit(1); if (data && data.length > 0 && data[0].to_no) { const parts = data[0].to_no.split('-'); const parsed = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10); if (!isNaN(parsed)) seq = parsed + 1; } return `${year}-${div}-${String(seq).padStart(5, '0')}`; } }; window.deletePendingTO = async function(toNo) { if(!confirm(`WARNING: Are you sure you want to DELETE Pending TO ${toNo} for ALL participants? This action cannot be undone.`)) return; await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', toNo); await supabase.from('travel_orders').delete().eq('to_no', toNo); await window.logActivity(toNo, 'DELETED_ALL', `Pending Travel Order completely deleted by ${currentUser.full_name}`); window.loadStatus(); }; window.withdrawPendingTO = async function(toNo) { if(!confirm(`Withdraw from Pending TO ${toNo}?\n\nYou will be removed from this travel, but other participants will remain unaffected.`)) return; const { data: others } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('emp_id').eq('to_no', toNo).neq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id); await supabase.from('travel_orders').delete().eq('to_no', toNo).eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id); if (!others || others.length === 0) { await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', toNo); } await window.logActivity(toNo, 'WITHDRAWN', `User ${currentUser.full_name} withdrew from pending TO`); window.loadStatus(); }; window.cancelApprovedTO = async function(toNo) { if(!confirm(`Cancel your participation in Approved TO ${toNo}? (Other participants will remain unaffected)`)) return; await supabase.from('travel_orders').update({ status: 'CANCELLED' }).eq('to_no', toNo).eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id); await supabase.from('notifications').insert({ recipient_role: 'PARPO', message: `Approved TO ${toNo} was cancelled for ${currentUser.full_name}` }); await window.logActivity(toNo, 'CANCELLED', 'Approved Travel Order Cancelled by ' + currentUser.full_name); window.loadStatus(); }; window.dropApprovedTODay = async function(toNo) { const { data: itin } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('travel_date').eq('to_no', toNo).order('travel_date'); if (!itin || itin.length === 0) return alert("No dates found."); const list = document.getElementById('drop-day-list'); list.innerHTML = ''; itin.forEach(day => { const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.className = 'btn btn-warning'; btn.style.margin = '0'; btn.style.color = 'white'; btn.innerText = `Drop ${day.travel_date}`; btn.onclick = () => window.executeDropDay(toNo, day.travel_date); list.appendChild(btn); }); document.getElementById('dropDayModal').style.display = 'flex'; }; window.executeDropDay = async function(toNo, targetDate) { if(!confirm(`Are you sure you want to drop ${targetDate}?`)) return; document.getElementById('dropDayModal').style.display = 'none'; // Check 2-day rule const target = new Date(targetDate.trim()); const today = new Date(todayStr); const diffDays = Math.ceil((target - today) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)); if (diffDays > 2) return alert("Dropping can only be done within 2 days prior to the Travel date."); const { data: itin } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo); if (!itin.some(i => i.travel_date === targetDate.trim())) return alert("Date not found in this TO."); if (itin.length === 1) { alert("This TO only has one day. Dropping it will cancel the entire TO for you."); return window.cancelApprovedTO(toNo); } const { data: fullGroup } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo); const othersGroup = fullGroup.filter(t => t.emp_id !== currentUser.emp_id); const divergingUser = fullGroup.find(t => t.emp_id === currentUser.emp_id); if (othersGroup.length === 0) { await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', toNo).eq('travel_date', targetDate.trim()); await window.logActivity(toNo, 'DAY_DROPPED', `Dropped ${targetDate.trim()} from TO`); alert(`Day ${targetDate.trim()} dropped successfully.`); window.loadStatus(); } else { alert("Group TO detected. The original TO will be cancelled and two new independent Travel Orders will be generated."); // 1. Void the Original await supabase.from('travel_orders').update({ status: 'CANCELLED' }).eq('to_no', toNo); // 2. Spawn Unaffected Group's TO const groupToNo = await window.generateToNo(othersGroup[0].division ? othersGroup[0].division.split(',')[0].trim() : "GEN"); const batchGroupTO = othersGroup.map(({ id, created_at, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, to_no: groupToNo, status: rest.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' ? 'READY_FOR_PRINT' : 'APPROVED' })); await supabase.from('travel_orders').insert(batchGroupTO); const batchGroupItin = itin.map(({ id, created_at, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, to_no: groupToNo })); await supabase.from('itinerary').insert(batchGroupItin); // 3. Spawn Diverging User's TO const soloToNo = await window.generateToNo(divergingUser.division ? divergingUser.division.split(',')[0].trim() : "GEN"); const { id: dId, created_at: dCa, ...soloRest } = divergingUser; await supabase.from('travel_orders').insert({ ...soloRest, to_no: soloToNo, status: soloRest.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' ? 'READY_FOR_PRINT' : 'APPROVED' }); const remainingDates = itin.filter(day => day.travel_date !== targetDate.trim()); const batchSoloItin = remainingDates.map(({ id, created_at, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, to_no: soloToNo })); await supabase.from('itinerary').insert(batchSoloItin); // 4. Log the Split await window.logActivity(toNo, 'SPLIT_CANCELLED', `Group TO split due to date drop by ${currentUser.full_name}`); await window.logActivity(groupToNo, 'SPLIT_CREATED', `Spawned from ${toNo} (Unaffected Group)`); await window.logActivity(soloToNo, 'SPLIT_CREATED', `Spawned from ${toNo} (Diverging User dropped ${targetDate.trim()})`); await supabase.from('notifications').insert({ emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, message: `Original TO ${toNo} split. Unaffected group moved to ${groupToNo}. Your new TO is ${soloToNo}.`, sender: 'System' }); alert(`Split successful. Your new TO number is ${soloToNo}`); window.loadStatus(); } }; window.modifyPendingTO = async function(toNo) { if(!confirm("Load this TO back into draft for modification?\n\nWARNING: Modifying this TO affects the ENTIRE GROUP. You will be able to change all details and remove personnel. Submitting will overwrite the original data for everyone. Proceed?")) return; const { data: toGroup } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo); const myTo = toGroup.find(t => t.emp_id === currentUser.emp_id); if (!myTo) return; // Load full group and preserve TO number editingToNo = toNo; personnelDraft = toGroup.map(g => ({ emp_id: g.emp_id, name: g.emp_name, unit: g.unit })); const { data: itin } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo).order('travel_date'); document.getElementById('to-transport').value = myTo.transport; document.getElementById('dep-time-group').style.display = myTo.transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE" ? 'block' : 'none'; document.getElementById('to-outside-bicol').checked = myTo.remarks && myTo.remarks.includes("OUTSIDE BICOL"); if (itin.length > 0) { document.getElementById('itin-from').value = itin[0].travel_date; document.getElementById('itin-to').value = itin[itin.length - 1].travel_date; } const container = document.getElementById('dynamic-itin-fields'); container.innerHTML = ''; itineraryDraft = []; const isPBDD = currentUser && String(currentUser.division).toUpperCase().includes('PBDD'); const userUnit = currentUser && currentUser.unit ? currentUser.unit.trim() : ''; // Travel Orders saved by this version retain a separate report-to for each date. // For older orders, fall back to the original undated report-to field. const reportByDate = new Map(); String(myTo.report_to || '').split(' | ').forEach(part => { const match = part.match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}) - ([\s\S]+)$/); if (match) reportByDate.set(match[1], match[2].trim()); }); let pbddOptions = ''; if (userUnit) pbddOptions += `<option value="${userUnit === 'NA' ? '' : userUnit}">${userUnit === 'NA' ? 'NA (Direct to Chief)' : userUnit + ' (Original Unit)'}</option>`; ['SIB/SMEDSS', 'EDES', 'CRFPS', 'NA'].forEach(u => { if (u !== userUnit) pbddOptions += `<option value="${u === 'NA' ? '' : u}">${u === 'NA' ? 'NA (Direct to Chief)' : u}</option>`; }); itin.forEach((i, idx) => { const cardId = `itin-card-${idx}`; // Fix: properly select the previously saved unit for PBDD modifications const savedUnitVal = (myTo.unit && myTo.unit !== 'NA') ? myTo.unit : ''; const pbddSelect = isPBDD ? `<select id="unit-${idx}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;">${pbddOptions.replace(`value="${savedUnitVal}"`, `value="${savedUnitVal}" selected`)}</select>` : ''; container.innerHTML += ` <div class="card" id="${cardId}" style="padding:10px; margin-bottom: 10px; box-shadow: none; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-left: 4px solid var(--dar-green);"> <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:8px;"> <h5 style="margin: 0; color: var(--dar-green); font-size: 13px;">📅 ${i.travel_date}</h5> <span style="color:var(--danger); font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.removeItinCard('${cardId}', '${i.travel_date}')">DELETE</span> </div> <input type="text" id="dest-${idx}" placeholder="Destination" value="${(i.destination || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;"> ${idx > 0 ? `<label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: normal; margin-bottom: 8px; color: var(--text-muted); cursor: pointer; white-space:nowrap;"><input type="checkbox" style="width: auto; margin: 0; padding: 0;" onchange="if(this.checked) document.getElementById('purp-${idx}').value = document.getElementById('purp-${idx-1}').value; else document.getElementById('purp-${idx}').value = '';"> Copy previous purpose</label>` : ''} <textarea id="purp-${idx}" rows="2" placeholder="Purpose" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;">${i.purpose}</textarea> <input type="text" id="fund-${idx}" list="fund-sources-list" placeholder="Fund Source (Select or Type)" value="${(myTo.fund_source || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:8px;"> ${pbddSelect} <input type="text" id="rep-${idx}" placeholder="Report To" value="${(reportByDate.get(i.travel_date) ?? (reportByDate.size ? '' : (myTo.report_to || ''))).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;')}" style="padding:8px; font-size:13px; margin-bottom:0;"> </div>`; itineraryDraft.push({ date: i.travel_date, destId: `dest-${idx}`, purpId: `purp-${idx}`, repId: `rep-${idx}`, fundId: `fund-${idx}`, unitId: isPBDD ? `unit-${idx}` : null, cardId: cardId, hasPSO: false }); }); window.renderPersonnel(); if (String(currentUser.s_assignment).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE') { const savedDivs = myTo.division.split(',').map(d => d.trim()); document.querySelectorAll('input[name="special_div"]').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = savedDivs.includes(cb.value); }); } alert(`Draft loaded for TO ${toNo}. Modifications will apply to ALL personnel listed.`); window.switchTab('apply-view', document.querySelector('.nav-item')); }; window.generateOfficialPDF = async function(toNo) { const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf; const doc = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4'); const getAssetUrl = (fileName) => `${supabaseUrl}/storage/v1/object/public/e-sign/${fileName}`; // HELPER: Groups dates into formats like "March 1-3 & 5, 2026" or "June 1, 2 & 5, 2026" const groupConsecutiveDates = (dates) => { if (!dates || !dates.length) return ""; const sorted = dates.map(d => new Date(d.replace(/-/g, '/'))).sort((a, b) => a - b); const monthYearGroups = {}; sorted.forEach(d => { const key = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); if (!monthYearGroups[key]) monthYearGroups[key] = []; monthYearGroups[key].push(d.getDate()); }); const result = []; for (const [monthYear, days] of Object.entries(monthYearGroups)) { let dayGroups = []; let tempGroup = [days[0]]; for (let i = 1; i < days.length; i++) { if (days[i] === days[i-1] + 1) { tempGroup.push(days[i]); } else { dayGroups.push(tempGroup); tempGroup = [days[i]]; } } dayGroups.push(tempGroup); const dayStrings = dayGroups.map(grp => { if (grp.length === 1) return `${grp[0]}`; if (grp.length === 2) return `${grp[0]}, ${grp[1]}`; return `${grp[0]}-${grp[grp.length - 1]}`; }); let daysStr = ""; if (dayStrings.length > 1) { const last = dayStrings.pop(); daysStr = dayStrings.join(', ') + ' & ' + last; } else { daysStr = dayStrings[0]; } const [month, year] = monthYear.split(' '); result.push(`${month} ${daysStr}, ${year}`); } return result.join('; '); }; try { const { data: group_orders } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo); const { data: itin_list } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo).order('travel_date'); const { data: emp_details } = await supabase.from('employees').select('emp_id, full_name, position, station, type'); // FETCH VEHICLE LOG SCHEDULE for red ink rendering const { data: vLog } = await supabase.from('vehicle_logs').select('schedule').eq('to_no', toNo).maybeSingle(); const od = group_orders[0]; const names = group_orders.map(m => m.emp_name.toUpperCase()).join(", "); const positions = [...new Set(group_orders.map(m => emp_details?.find(e => e.emp_id === m.emp_id)?.position || ""))].join(", ").toUpperCase(); const stations = [...new Set(group_orders.map(m => emp_details?.find(e => e.emp_id === m.emp_id)?.station || ""))].join(", ").toUpperCase(); const rawType = emp_details?.find(e => e.emp_id === group_orders[0].emp_id)?.type?.toUpperCase(); const typeLabel = rawType === 'SPLIT' ? 'SPLIT/COS' : (rawType === 'REGULAR' ? 'REGULAR' : ''); const isMARPO = currentUser.position && String(currentUser.position).toUpperCase().includes("MARPO"); // Get Unique Units and Divisions from the Personnel in this TO const rawUnits = group_orders.flatMap(o => o.unit ? o.unit.split(',').map(u=>u.trim()) : []).filter(u => u && u !== "PARPO" && u !== "NA"); const uniqueUnits = [...new Set(rawUnits)]; const uniqueDivs = [...new Set(group_orders.flatMap(o => o.division ? o.division.split(',').map(d=>d.trim()) : []).filter(d => d))]; let uh_list = []; if (!isMARPO && uniqueUnits.length > 0) { const { data } = await supabase.from('users').select('full_name, username').in('unit', uniqueUnits); if (data) uh_list = data; } let dc_list = []; if (currentUser.unit !== "PARPO" && uniqueDivs.length > 0) { const { data } = await supabase.from('users').select('full_name, username, role').in('division', uniqueDivs).in('role', ['CHIEF', 'PARPO I', 'PARPO_I', 'PARPO1', 'PARPO_1']); if (data) dc_list = data.map(u => ({ ...u, custom_role: ['PARPO I', 'PARPO_I', 'PARPO1', 'PARPO_1'].includes(u.role?.toUpperCase()) ? "PARPO I" : "DIVISION CHIEF" })); } // ADDED: For ARBDSP orders, include PARPO I under Recommending Approval const isARBDSP = od.division?.toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP") || od.unit?.toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP") || od.fund_source?.toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP"); if (isARBDSP && !dc_list.some(dc => dc.custom_role === "PARPO I")) { const { data: p1Data } = await supabase.from('users').select('full_name, username').in('role', ['PARPO_I', 'PARPO I', 'PARPO1', 'PARPO_1']).limit(1); if (p1Data && p1Data.length > 0) { dc_list.push({ ...p1Data[0], custom_role: "PARPO I" }); } else if (od.parpo1_signed) { // Fallback if role lookup fails but signature name was recorded dc_list.push({ full_name: od.parpo1_signed, username: "", custom_role: "PARPO I" }); } } // Keep the source JPEG's original pixels and compression. No canvas scaling, // re-encoding, or generated QR code is used in the Travel Order PDF. const img_bg = await urlToBase64(getAssetUrl("bg_form.jpg")); const img_p2 = null; const uh_imgs = []; const dc_imgs = []; if (img_bg) { try { doc.addImage(img_bg, 'JPEG', 0, 0, 210, 297); } catch(e){ console.warn('Unable to render original stationery:', e); } } let topY = 8; // TOP MOST OVERRIDE FOR TO HEADER if (od.to_header) { doc.setTextColor(255, 0, 0); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(9); doc.text(od.to_header.toUpperCase(), 105, topY, { align: 'center' }); doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0); topY += 4; } if (od.transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE") { doc.setTextColor(255, 0, 0); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(9); doc.text("USED GOVERNMENT VEHICLE, SUBJECT TO TEV CLAIM VALIDATION", 105, topY, { align: 'center' }); doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0); topY += 4; } // Preserve the employee category in its original location, without a QR image. if (typeLabel) { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(8); doc.text(typeLabel, 190, 33, {align: 'center'}); } if (od.remarks && od.remarks.includes("EXISTING PSO")) { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(9); doc.text("WITH EXISTING PSO/RSO", 105, 28, { align: 'center' }); } // Do not display the database TO reference or its number field on the printed form. doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(14); doc.text("TRAVEL ORDER", 105, 35, { align: 'center' }); const fDate = new Date(od.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text(`Date: ${fDate}`, 105, 43, { align: 'center' }); const renderFlexibleText = (text, x, y, maxWidth, lineEndX, defaultSize, maxLines = 2) => { let currentSize = defaultSize; doc.setFontSize(currentSize); let lines = doc.splitTextToSize(text || "", maxWidth); while (lines.length > maxLines && currentSize > 4.5) { currentSize -= 0.5; doc.setFontSize(currentSize); lines = doc.splitTextToSize(text || "", maxWidth); } let curY = y; lines.forEach((line) => { doc.text(line, x, curY); doc.line(x, curY + 1, lineEndX, curY + 1); curY += 4.5; }); doc.setFontSize(defaultSize); return lines.length; }; doc.setFontSize(10); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("NAME:", 20, 48); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); let nameLinesCount = renderFlexibleText(names, 40, 48, 135, 175, 10, 4); let nextY = 48 + ((nameLinesCount - 1) * 4.5) + 6; doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("POSITION:", 20, nextY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); let posLinesCount = renderFlexibleText(positions, 40, nextY, 45, 85, 10, 2); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("STATION:", 90, nextY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); let statLinesCount = renderFlexibleText(stations, 110, nextY, 65, 175, 10, 2); let maxSubLines = Math.max(posLinesCount, statLinesCount); let transY = nextY + ((maxSubLines - 1) * 4.5) + 7; doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("MEANS OF TRANSPORTATION:", 20, transY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(9); const drawCheck = (label, x, y, isChecked) => { doc.rect(x, y - 3, 3, 3); if (isChecked) { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("X", x + 0.5, y - 0.2); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); } doc.text(label, x + 5, y); }; drawCheck("AIR", 20, transY + 5, od.transport === "AIR"); drawCheck("GOV'T VEHICLE", 70, transY + 5, od.transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE"); if (od.transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE" && vLog && vLog.schedule) { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(8); doc.setTextColor(255, 0, 0); doc.text(`Sched: ${vLog.schedule}`, 103, transY + 5); doc.setTextColor(0, 0, 0); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); } drawCheck("LAND", 20, transY + 10, od.transport === "LAND"); drawCheck("OTHERS, PLEASE SPECIFY", 70, transY + 10, od.transport === "OTHERS"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); let destLabelY = transY + 17; doc.text("DESTINATION:", 20, destLabelY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(7); let destY = destLabelY; const destMaxWidth = 125; // Grouping Same Destinations Over Same/Consecutive Dates const destMap = new Map(); itin_list.forEach(it => { if (!destMap.has(it.destination)) destMap.set(it.destination, []); destMap.get(it.destination).push(it.travel_date); }); destMap.forEach((dates, dest) => { const formattedDates = groupConsecutiveDates(dates); const fullDestText = `${formattedDates} - ${dest}`; const wrappedDestLines = doc.splitTextToSize(fullDestText, destMaxWidth); wrappedDestLines.forEach((line) => { doc.text(line, 50, destY); destY += 3.5; }); }); let curY = Math.max(destY, destLabelY + 5); // Sort explicitly to guarantee earliest and latest are accurate const sortedItin = [...itin_list].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.travel_date) - new Date(b.travel_date)); let earliestDate = sortedItin[0]?.travel_date || ""; let latestDate = sortedItin[sortedItin.length - 1]?.travel_date || ""; let isContinuous = true; if (sortedItin.length > 1) { for (let i = 0; i < sortedItin.length - 1; i++) { let d1 = new Date(sortedItin[i].travel_date); let d2 = new Date(sortedItin[i+1].travel_date); let diff = Math.round((d2 - d1) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)); if (diff !== 1) { isContinuous = false; break; } } } let departureDateStr = earliestDate; let returnDateStr = latestDate; if (!isContinuous && sortedItin.length > 1) { departureDateStr = groupConsecutiveDates(sortedItin.map(i => i.travel_date)); returnDateStr = "PM OF EACH DATE"; } doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text("DEPARTURE DATE:", 20, curY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(departureDateStr.length > 15 ? 6 : 10); // Shrinks font if string is long so it fits the line doc.text(departureDateStr, 55, curY); doc.line(55, curY+1, 90, curY+1); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text("RETURN DATE:", 95, curY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(returnDateStr.length > 15 ? 7 : 10); doc.text(returnDateStr, 125, curY); doc.line(125, curY+1, 175, curY+1); curY += 6; doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("REPORT TO:", 20, curY); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.text(od.report_to || "", 50, curY); doc.line(50, curY+1, 175, curY+1); curY += 6; doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text("PURPOSE OF TRAVEL:", 20, curY); curY += 4; // Grouping Same Purposes Over Same/Consecutive Dates const purpMap = new Map(); itin_list.forEach(it => { if (!it.purpose) return; if (!purpMap.has(it.purpose)) purpMap.set(it.purpose, []); purpMap.get(it.purpose).push(it.travel_date); }); let finalPurposeStr = ""; if (purpMap.size === 1) { // If there's only 1 unique purpose, summarize it without dating finalPurposeStr = Array.from(purpMap.keys())[0]; } else { // Mixed purposes: group by consecutive dates let groupedPurposes = []; purpMap.forEach((dates, purp) => { const formattedDates = groupConsecutiveDates(dates); groupedPurposes.push(`${formattedDates} - ${purp}`); }); finalPurposeStr = groupedPurposes.join('; '); } let purpFontSize = 9; doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(purpFontSize); let purpLines = doc.splitTextToSize(finalPurposeStr || "", 155); while (purpLines.length > 3 && purpFontSize > 5) { purpFontSize -= 0.5; doc.setFontSize(purpFontSize); purpLines = doc.splitTextToSize(finalPurposeStr || "", 155); } purpLines.forEach((line) => { doc.text(line, 20, curY); doc.line(20, curY + 1, 175, curY + 1); curY += 4.5; }); curY += 2; doc.setFont("helvetica", "italic"); doc.setFontSize(8); doc.text("In case of seminar/workshop/consultation/briefing, indicate:", 20, curY); drawCheck("LIVE IN", 110, curY, false); drawCheck("LIVE OUT", 135, curY, false); const renderSigs = (list, imgs, startX, width, roleLabel) => { if (list.length === 0) return; const count = list.length; const step = width / count; const imgSize = count > 2 ? 18 : 25; for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) { let cx = startX + (step * j) + (step / 2); let imgX = cx - (imgSize / 2); if (imgs[j]) { try { doc.addImage(imgs[j], 'PNG', imgX, 155, imgSize, imgSize); } catch(e){} } let name = list[j].full_name.toUpperCase(); let fontSize = 8; doc.setFontSize(fontSize); while (doc.getTextWidth(name) > (step - 2) && fontSize > 4) { fontSize -= 0.5; doc.setFontSize(fontSize); } doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.text(name, cx, 171, { align: 'center' }); let lineStart = cx - (step / 2) + 2; let lineEnd = cx + (step / 2) - 2; doc.line(lineStart, 173, lineEnd, 173); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(7); doc.text(roleLabel, cx, 177, { align: 'center' }); } }; if (od.remarks && od.remarks.includes("OUTSIDE BICOL")) { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text("Recommending Approval:", 20, 156); // Division Chief Signatures (Dynamically handles multiple) renderSigs(dc_list, dc_imgs, 20, 75, "DIVISION CHIEF"); // Force aligned Signature positions mapping exactly to renderSigs Y-axes (Y=171, 173, 177) if(img_p2) { try { doc.addImage(img_p2, 'PNG', 117, 153, 20, 20); } catch(e){} } doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(8); doc.text("RICARDO C. GARCIA", 127, 171, {align: 'center'}); doc.line(100, 173, 155, 173); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(7); doc.text("PARPO II", 127, 177, {align: 'center'}); doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(9.5); doc.text("RODRIGO O. REALUBIT, CESO VI", 114, 202); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(9); doc.text("Regional Director", 128, 206); } else { doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text("Recommended by:", 20, 156); // Dynamically splits space for Unit Heads and Chiefs if (!isMARPO) renderSigs(uh_list, uh_imgs, 15, 80, "MARPO / UNIT HEAD"); renderSigs(dc_list, dc_imgs, 100, 80, "DIVISION CHIEF"); // Scaled PARPO II Signature to 24x24 instead of 30x30 if(img_p2) { try { doc.addImage(img_p2, 'PNG', 117, 185, 24, 24); } catch(e){} } doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(11); doc.text("RICARDO C. GARCIA", 114, 202); doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(9); doc.text("PARPO II", 128, 206); } await window.saveSecurePDF(doc, `TO_${toNo}_Secure.pdf`); } catch (e) { alert("PDF Error: " + e.message); } }; // Form source: user-supplied "Copy of TRAVEL ORDER FORM.xlsx - TO.pdf". // Native PDF objects are retained: logos, borders, column sizing, type, spacing and footer. // Only the window.generateOfficialPDFNewFormat = async function(toNo) { try { if (!window.PDFLib || !window.PDFLib.PDFDocument) { throw new Error('PDF library is unavailable. Connect to the internet and reload the page.'); } const [orderResult, itinResult, peopleResult] = await Promise.all([ supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo), supabase.from('itinerary').select('*').eq('to_no', toNo).order('travel_date'), supabase.from('employees').select('emp_id,full_name,position,station,type') ]); if (orderResult.error) throw orderResult.error; if (itinResult.error) throw itinResult.error; if (!orderResult.data || !orderResult.data.length) throw new Error('Travel Order record not found.'); const orders = orderResult.data; const itin = (itinResult.data || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.travel_date).localeCompare(String(b.travel_date))); const employeeRecords = peopleResult.data || []; const order = orders[0]; const lookup = id => employeeRecords.find(e=>String(e.emp_id)===String(id)) || {}; const upper = v => String(v || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const distinct = vals => [...new Set(vals.map(v => String(v||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))]; const names = distinct(orders.map(o=>upper(o.emp_name || lookup(o.emp_id).full_name))).join(', '); const positions = distinct(orders.map(o=>upper(lookup(o.emp_id).position))).join(', '); const stations = distinct(orders.map(o=>upper(lookup(o.emp_id).station))).join(', '); const longDate = value => { const match = String(value || '').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); return match ? new Date(Date.UTC(+match[1], +match[2]-1, +match[3])).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month:'long', day:'numeric', year:'numeric', timeZone:'UTC'}) : String(value || ''); }; // Legacy TOs have one combined Report To; newer records store a dated list. const reportByDate = new Map(); String(order.report_to || '').split(' | ').forEach(part => { const m = part.match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}) - ([\s\S]+)$/); if(m)reportByDate.set(m[1],m[2].trim()); }); const dateRows = (field, legacyValue='') => itin.map(row => longDate(row.travel_date) + ' - ' + (field==='report_to' ? (reportByDate.size ? (reportByDate.get(row.travel_date) || '') : legacyValue) : (row[field] || '')) ).filter(v=>!v.endsWith(' - ')); const neatDate = v => { if(!v) return ''; const m=String(v).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d\d)-(\d\d)/); if(m) return new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1],+m[2]-1,+m[3])).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric',timeZone:'UTC'}); const d=new Date(v); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? String(v) : d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric',timeZone:'Asia/Manila'}); }; const spanDates = dates => { const a=distinct(dates.filter(Boolean)).sort(); if (!a.length) return ''; return a.length===1 ? neatDate(a[0]) : neatDate(a[0])+' - '+neatDate(a[a.length-1]); }; // Load the exact PDF in its original A4 size; do not rasterize or re-encode. const pdfBytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(DAR_TRAVEL_ORDER_EXACT_PDF_BASE64),c=>c.charCodeAt(0)); const pdf=await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(pdfBytes); const page=pdf.getPage(0); const formFont=await pdf.embedFont(PDFLib.StandardFonts.Helvetica); const formBold=await pdf.embedFont(PDFLib.StandardFonts.HelveticaBold); const black=PDFLib.rgb(0,0,0); const H=page.getHeight(); const safe=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[\u2010-\u2015]/g,'-').replace(/[\u2018\u2019]/g,"'").replace(/[\u201c\u201d]/g,'"').replace(/[^\x20-\xFF]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); // Input coordinates are original-PDF points measured from the top of the A4 page. const write=(value,x,y,maxWidth=200,size=8.4,bold=false,align='left')=>{ const txt=safe(value); if(!txt)return; const f=bold?formBold:formFont; let sz=size; while(sz>5.2 && f.widthOfTextAtSize(txt,sz)>maxWidth)sz-=0.2; let rendered=txt; if(f.widthOfTextAtSize(rendered,sz)>maxWidth){ while(rendered.length && f.widthOfTextAtSize(rendered+'...',sz)>maxWidth)rendered=rendered.slice(0,-1); rendered+='...'; } const w=f.widthOfTextAtSize(rendered,sz); const xpos=align==='center'?x-w/2:align==='right'?x-w:x; page.drawText(rendered,{x:xpos,y:H-y,font:f,size:sz,color:black}); }; const words=(value,maxWidth,size=8.2,maxLines=3,bold=false)=>{ const f=bold?formBold:formFont; const tokens=safe(value).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const lines=[];let line=''; for(const token of tokens){ const candidate=line?line+' '+token:token; if(line && f.widthOfTextAtSize(candidate,size)>maxWidth){lines.push(line);line=token;} else line=candidate; } if(line)lines.push(line); if(lines.length>maxLines){const result=lines.slice(0,maxLines); result[maxLines-1]=lines.slice(maxLines-1).join(' '); return result; } return lines; }; const textLines=(value,x,y,maxWidth,lineHeight,maxLines=3,size=8.2,bold=false)=>{ const arr=words(value,maxWidth,size,maxLines,bold); arr.forEach((s,i)=>write(s,x,y+i*lineHeight,maxWidth,size,bold)); }; const mark=(x,y)=>write('X',x,y,13,12,true); // Preserve the user's original, one-page A4 form. If any of its small // ruled fields cannot contain all dated entries at a legible size, // refer to a complete attached itinerary instead of rejecting/truncating. const fullyWrapped = (input, maxWidth, fontSize, font=formFont) => { const lines=[]; const words=safe(input).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); let current=''; for(const token of words){ // Break even unspaced long strings rather than clip or append ellipses. const pieces=[]; let piece=''; for(const char of token){ if(piece && font.widthOfTextAtSize(piece+char,fontSize)>maxWidth){pieces.push(piece);piece=char;} else piece+=char; } if(piece)pieces.push(piece); for(let n=0;n<pieces.length;n++){ const part=pieces[n]; const candidate=current ? current+' '+part : part; if(current && font.widthOfTextAtSize(candidate,fontSize)>maxWidth){ lines.push(current); current=part; } else current=candidate; if(n<pieces.length-1){lines.push(current);current='';} } } if(current)lines.push(current); return lines; }; const fitDatedField = (rows,baselines,width,originalSize=8.0) => { const input=rows.map(safe).filter(Boolean); if(!input.length)return {size:originalSize,lines:[]}; // Lower bound is intentionally readable; details move to the // continuation page rather than shrinking to microprint. for(let sz=originalSize;sz>=7.0;sz-=0.2){ const lines=input.flatMap(item=>fullyWrapped(item,width,sz)); if(lines.length<=baselines.length)return {size:sz,lines}; } return null; }; const drawDatedFit=(fit,x,baselines,width)=>{ if(!fit)return; fit.lines.forEach((line,i)=>write(line,x,baselines[i],width,fit.size)); }; const datedDestination=dateRows('destination'); const datedReport=dateRows('report_to',order.report_to || ''); const datedPurpose=dateRows('purpose'); const destinationFit=fitDatedField(datedDestination,[300,309],395,8.0); const reportFit=fitDatedField(datedReport,[365,374],418,8.0); const purposeFit=fitDatedField(datedPurpose,[414,433,453],498,8.2); const needsContinuation=!(destinationFit && reportFit && purposeFit); const continuationLabel='SEE ATTACHED DATED ITINERARY ('+itin.length+' DAY'+(itin.length===1?'':'S')+')'; // Page 2 onward is a complete, date-by-date continuation, only when // needed. It is fully paginated and neither changes the official form // on page 1 nor its existing signatories and footer. const addDatedContinuation = () => { const margin=37; const usable=page.getWidth()-margin*2; const lineGap=12.6; let sheet, cursor; const sheetText=(v,x,top,size=9,font=formFont,color=black)=>{ if(!v)return; sheet.drawText(safe(v),{x,y:sheet.getHeight()-top,font,size,color}); }; const newSheet=()=>{ sheet=pdf.addPage([page.getWidth(),page.getHeight()]); cursor=42; sheetText('REPUBLIC OF THE PHILIPPINES',margin,cursor,8.5,formBold); sheetText('DEPARTMENT OF AGRARIAN REFORM',margin,cursor+13,9.5,formBold); sheetText('TRAVEL ORDER - DATED ITINERARY (CONTINUATION)',margin,cursor+37,12.5,formBold); sheetText('Integral attachment to the Travel Order on the preceding form.',margin,cursor+54,8.4); sheet.drawLine({start:{x:margin,y:sheet.getHeight()-cursor-66},end:{x:margin+usable,y:sheet.getHeight()-cursor-66},thickness:0.7,color:PDFLib.rgb(0.3,0.3,0.3)}); cursor+=84; }; const space=(height)=>{ if(!sheet || cursor+height>page.getHeight()-55)newSheet(); }; const field=(label,value)=>{ const content=fullyWrapped(value||'-',usable-124,9.2); const lines=content.length?content:['-']; let at=0; while(at<lines.length){ space(25); const capacity=Math.floor((page.getHeight()-55-cursor-5)/lineGap); if(capacity<1){newSheet();continue;} const count=Math.min(capacity,lines.length-at); sheetText(at===0?label:label+' (cont.)',margin+10,cursor,8.3,formBold); for(let i=0;i<count;i++)sheetText(lines[at+i],margin+119,cursor+i*lineGap,9.2); cursor+=count*lineGap+9; at+=count; } }; for(const day of itin){ space(107); const date=longDate(day.travel_date); sheet.drawRectangle({x:margin,y:sheet.getHeight()-cursor-14,width:usable,height:21,color:PDFLib.rgb(0.94,0.96,0.97)}); sheetText(date,margin+9,cursor,10,formBold); cursor+=32; field('DESTINATION:',day.destination||''); field('REPORT TO:',reportByDate.size ? (reportByDate.get(day.travel_date)||'') : (order.report_to||'')); field('PURPOSE OF TRAVEL:',day.purpose||''); cursor+=10; } // Page counts can only be determined after all continuation pages exist. const count=pdf.getPageCount(); pdf.getPages().slice(1).forEach((p,i)=>{ p.drawLine({start:{x:margin,y:37},end:{x:page.getWidth()-margin,y:37},thickness:0.5,color:PDFLib.rgb(0.6,0.6,0.6)}); p.drawText('TRAVEL ORDER - CONTINUATION SHEET',{ x:margin,y:24,font:formFont,size:7.6,color:black }); const pageLabel='Page '+(i+2)+' of '+count; p.drawText(pageLabel,{x:page.getWidth()-margin-formFont.widthOfTextAtSize(pageLabel,7.6),y:24,font:formFont,size:7.6,color:black}); }); }; // Erase only the sample form's gray placeholder names beneath signature lines. // The original boxes, signature lines and headings remain intact. const replaceSignaturePlaceholder = (x,top,width,height)=>page.drawRectangle({ x,y:H-top-height,width,height,color:PDFLib.rgb(1,1,1),borderWidth:0 }); const signatory = (person,position,x,top,maxWidth,gap=9)=>{ const name=upper(person);if(!name)return; write(name,x,top,maxWidth,7.8,true,'center'); if(position)write(upper(position),x,top+gap,maxWidth,6.5,false,'center'); }; // Fields are overlaid INSIDE the unmodified grid/ruled lines of the source PDF. write(neatDate(order.created_at),137,165,144,8.6); // No printed TO number, no generated QR and no date of electronic approval. write(names,138,214,143,8.3,true); write(positions,138,228,143,8.0); write(stations,389,214,175,8.3); const tr=upper(order.transport); if(tr==='AIR') mark(191,270); else if(tr==='WATER') mark(236,270); else if(tr==='LAND') mark(294,270); else if(tr.includes('VEHICLE')) mark(345,270); else {mark(468,270); if(tr!=='OTHERS')write(order.transport,530,270,30,6.8);} if(needsContinuation)write(continuationLabel,160,300,395,8.0); else drawDatedFit(destinationFit,160,[300,309],395); write(spanDates(itin.length ? [itin[0].travel_date] : []),151,339,125,8.1); write(spanDates(itin.length ? [itin[itin.length-1].travel_date] : []),393,339,171,8.1); if(needsContinuation)write(continuationLabel,138,365,418,8.0); else drawDatedFit(reportFit,138,[365,374],418); // The original purpose box has exactly three lines; include the date per activity. if(needsContinuation)write(continuationLabel,48,414,498,8.2); else drawDatedFit(purposeFit,48,[414,433,453],498); const fund=upper(order.fund_source).split(',').map(v=>v.trim()).filter(Boolean); const matches=name=>fund.includes(name); if(matches('GAS'))mark(154,497); if(matches('LTSP'))mark(264,497); if(matches('AARC'))mark(390,497); if(fund.some(x=>['ALI/PALA','ALI','PALA'].includes(x)))mark(154,523); const requesterName=currentUser?.full_name || loggedEmployee.full_name || order.emp_name || ''; const requesterPosition=currentUser?.position || loggedEmployee.position || ''; const ownUnit=String(currentUser?.unit || order.unit || '').trim(); const ownDivision=String(currentUser?.division || order.division || '').split(',')[0].trim(); const units=distinct(ownUnit.split(',').map(v=>v.trim()).filter(v=>v && !['NA','PARPO'].includes(upper(v)))); let unitHead=[]; if(units.length){ const response=await supabase.from('users').select('full_name,role,unit') .in('unit',units).in('role',['UNIT_HEAD','UNIT HEAD','MARPO']); if(response.error)throw response.error; signatory(requesterName,requesterPosition,162,618,220); replaceSignaturePlaceholder(105,664,120,10); if(unitHead.length){ signatory(unitHead.join(' / '),'UNIT HEAD',162,666,220,6); } if(divisionChief.length){ replaceSignaturePlaceholder(349,652,180,11); signatory(divisionChief.join(' / '),'DIVISION CHIEF',437,657,250); } replaceSignaturePlaceholder(361,710,152,11); signatory('RICARDO C. GARCIA','PARPO II',437,714,245); // The official first page remains the unchanged sample; overflow adds sheets. if(needsContinuation)addDatedContinuation(); const output=await pdf.save({useObjectStreams:false}); const blob=new Blob([output],{type:'application/pdf'}); const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob); const link=document.createElement('a'); link.href=url;link.download='TRAVEL_ORDER_'+String(toNo).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_')+'.pdf'; document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),30000); }catch(error){console.error('Exact-form Travel Order generation failed',error);alert('Travel Order print error: '+error.message);} }; window.cancelLeaveApp = async function(leaveId) { if(!confirm("Are you sure you want to cancel this leave application?")) return; try { const { data: leaveApp, error: fetchError } = await supabase .from('leave_ledger') .select('start_date, created_at, remarks, type, transaction_type, debit') .eq('emp_id',currentUser.emp_id) .eq('id', leaveId) .single(); if (fetchError) throw fetchError; const nowPHT=getPSTDate(); // Employee may cancel up to and including the first leave day. // After the start date, only HR may cancel on employee request. if (!leaveApp.start_date || nowPHT > leaveApp.start_date) { alert('Self-cancellation is allowed through the leave start date (Philippine time). After that, request HR cancellation.'); return; } if (!/^(APPLICATION|PENDING|APPROVED|REQUESTED|SUBMITTED)/i.test(String(leaveApp.transaction_type||''))) { alert('This application is already cancelled, disapproved, or otherwise closed.'); if(delErr)throw delErr; } } } const {error:splitVlErr}=await supabase.from('leave_ledger').delete() .eq('emp_id',currentUser.emp_id).eq('type','Vacation Leave').eq('transaction_type','DEDUCTION') .ilike('remarks',`%SPL_WL_VL_FALLBACK_FOR_LEAVE_ID:${leaveId}%`); window.loadStatus(); } catch(e) { alert("Error: " + e.message); } }; window.loadStatus = async function() { const filterNode = document.querySelector('input[name="statusFilter"]:checked'); const filter = filterNode ? filterNode.value : "TO"; const list = document.getElementById('status-list'); list.innerHTML = "Fetching..."; if(filter === "TO") { const { data: tos } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id).not('status', 'ilike', '%DISAPPROVED%').order('created_at', {ascending: false}).limit(30); 'bg-cancelled' : 'bg-pending'); const isApproved = to.status === 'APPROVED', isPending = to.status.includes('PENDING'); const isPrintable = isReady || isApproved; html += `<div class="card" style="padding: 10px; margin-bottom: 10px;"> <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 8px;"> <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size: 13px;">${to.to_no}</div> <span class="badge ${sCls}">${isReady ? 'PRE-ONLINE RECORDED' : (app.transaction_type === 'APPLICATION' && routingLabel ? routingLabel : app.transaction_type)}</span> </div> <div style="font-size: 11px; margin-bottom: 4px;"><strong>Dates:</strong> ${app.start_date} to ${app.end_date} (${app.debit} days)</div> <div style="font-size: 10px; color: gray;"><strong>Remarks:</strong> ${app.remarks}</div> <div class="action-group" style="margin-top: 8px;"> ${historical ?
