' (Disapproved VL)' : ''}</button>` : '')} ${(!historical && !isCancelled && !cannotCancel) ? `<button class="btn btn-danger btn-small" onclick="window.cancelLeaveApp('${app.id}')">Cancel Leave</button>` : ''} </div> </div>`; } list.innerHTML = html || "No Leave Applications found."; } }; window.retractLeaveApp = async function(leaveId) { const {data:app,error:err}=await supabase.from('leave_ledger').select('*') .eq('id',leaveId).eq('emp_id',currentUser.emp_id).maybeSingle(); if(err || !app)return alert('Application not found.'); if(!app.start_date || getPSTDate()>app.start_date) return alert('After the leave start date, request cancellation from HR.'); if(String(app.transaction_type).toUpperCase()!=='APPROVED') return alert('Only approved leave can be retracted.'); const daysToRetract = prompt(`Approved Leave is ${app.debit} days.\nHow many days do you want to retract?`); if(!daysToRetract || isNaN(daysToRetract) || parseFloat(daysToRetract) <= 0 || parseFloat(daysToRetract) > app.debit) return alert("Invalid number of days."); if(confirm(`Retract ${daysToRetract} days from this leave? This will refund your credits automatically.`)) { await supabase.from('leave_ledger').insert({ transaction_id: crypto.randomUUID(), emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, start_date: todayStr, end_date: todayStr, type: app.type, credit: parseFloat(daysToRetract), transaction_type: "CREDIT", remarks: `Retracted ${daysToRetract} days from leave` }); if (parseFloat(daysToRetract) == app.debit) { await supabase.from('leave_ledger').update({ transaction_type: 'CANCELLED', remarks: (app.remarks || '') + ' | FULLY RETRACTED' }).eq('id', leaveId); } else { await supabase.from('leave_ledger').update({ debit: app.debit - parseFloat(daysToRetract), remarks: (app.remarks || '') + ` | RETRACTED ${daysToRetract} days` }).eq('id', leaveId); } alert("Leave successfully retracted and credits refunded."); window.loadStatus(); } }; // CTO CREDIT STATUS HELPERS — matched to the dedicated CTO Processing page. window.isCtoPendingCreditRecord = function(row) { const ttype = String(row?.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const rmk = String(row?.remarks || '').toUpperCase().trim(); return ['APPLICATION', 'PENDING', 'REQUESTED', 'SUBMITTED'].includes(ttype) || ['FOR HR PROCESSING', 'ENCODED', 'PENDING', 'APPLICATION', 'FOR PROCESSING'].includes(rmk); }; window.isCtoDisapprovedCreditRecord = function(row) { const ttype = String(row?.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const rmk = String(row?.remarks || '').toUpperCase().trim(); return ['DISAPPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'CANCELLED'].includes(ttype) || rmk.startsWith('DISAPPROVED') || rmk.startsWith('REJECTED') || rmk.startsWith('CANCELLED'); }; // The CTO Processing page treats EARNED as FINALIZED. window.isCtoFinalizedEarnedRecord = function(row) { const ttype = String(row?.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); return ttype === 'EARNED' && !window.isCtoPendingCreditRecord(row) && !window.isCtoDisapprovedCreditRecord(row); }; window.getCtoExpiryDate = function(dateStr) { const m = String(dateStr || '').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); if (!m) return ''; const d = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]))); d.setUTCFullYear(d.getUTCFullYear() + 1); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }; window.isCtoExpiredRecord = function(row) { if (!window.isCtoFinalizedEarnedRecord(row)) return false; const expiry = window.getCtoExpiryDate(row?.date); return !!expiry && getPSTDate() >= expiry; }; window.isCtoCreditedEarnedRecord = function(row) { return window.isCtoFinalizedEarnedRecord(row) && !window.isCtoExpiredRecord(row); }; window.isCtoUsedRecord = function(row) { const ttype = String(row?.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const rmk = String(row?.remarks || '').toUpperCase().trim(); return ttype === 'USED' || ttype === 'CTO DEDUCTION' || rmk === 'CTO DEDUCTION'; }; // CTO LEAVE HOURS HELPER // CTO is consumed in HOURS. employees.cto_balance is the opening/baseline // balance already carried over by HR before transition to this system. window.extractCtoHoursFromRemarks = function(remarks, fallbackDays = 0) { const match = String(remarks || '').match(/CTO HOURS:\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/i); if (match) return Math.abs(parseFloat(match[1]) || 0); return (Math.abs(parseFloat(fallbackDays || 0)) || 0) * 8; }; // For mixed CTO + Vacation Leave applications, only this VL portion is // charged against the employee's Vacation Leave balance. window.extractVlChargeFromRemarks = function(remarks, fallbackDays = 0) { const match = String(remarks || '').match(/VL CHARGE:\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/i); if (match) return Math.abs(parseFloat(match[1]) || 0); const upper = String(remarks || '').toUpperCase(); if (upper.includes('CREDIT TO CTO') || upper.includes('CREDITED TO CTO')) return 0; return Math.abs(parseFloat(fallbackDays || 0)) || 0; }; // Existing installations have used both schedule_override and schedule_overrides. // Read the current table first and fall back to the plural name. Do not require // created_at because older schedule tables may not have that column. window.fetchLeaveScheduleOverrides = async function(start = null, end = null) { const columns = 'emp_id, start_date, end_date, start_time, end_time, schedule_type'; const runQuery = async (tableName) => { let q = supabase.from(tableName) .select(columns) .in('emp_id', [currentUser.emp_id, 'ALL']); if (start && end) q = q.gte('end_date', start).lte('start_date', end); q = q.order('start_date', { ascending: false }); return await q; }; let result = await runQuery('schedule_override'); if (!result.error) return result.data || []; console.warn('schedule_override lookup failed; trying schedule_overrides:', result.error.message); result = await runQuery('schedule_overrides'); if (!result.error) return result.data || []; // CTO must remain usable even when there is no override table/row. // No override means the regular 8-hour schedule applies. console.warn('No readable schedule override table. Using standard schedule fallback:', result.error.message); return []; }; // CTO SCHEDULE HELPERS // schedule_override is the source of truth for the employee's current work schedule. // CTO is ALWAYS computed in HOURS. A FIXED 07:00-18:00 schedule is the current // compressed work week (10 working hours after the 12:00-13:00 lunch break). window.getCtoScheduleHours = function(override) { if (!override || String(override.schedule_type || '').toUpperCase() !== 'FIXED' || !override.start_time || !override.end_time) return 8; const toMins = (t) => { const p = String(t).split(':'); return (parseInt(p[0], 10) || 0) * 60 + (parseInt(p[1], 10) || 0); }; const startMins = toMins(override.start_time); const endMins = toMins(override.end_time); if (endMins <= startMins) return 8; let workMins = endMins - startMins; const lunchStart = 12 * 60; const lunchEnd = 13 * 60; const lunchOverlap = Math.max(0, Math.min(endMins, lunchEnd) - Math.max(startMins, lunchStart)); workMins -= lunchOverlap; return workMins > 0 ? workMins / 60 : 8; }; window.isCtoCompressedWorkWeek = function(override) { if (!override || String(override.schedule_type || '').toUpperCase() !== 'FIXED') return false; const startTime = String(override.start_time || '').substring(0, 5); const endTime = String(override.end_time || '').substring(0, 5); return startTime === '07:00' && endTime === '18:00'; }; window.getCtoRequiredHours = async function(start, end) { if (!start || !end) return { totalHours: 0, dates: [] }; const schedOverrides = await window.fetchLeaveScheduleOverrides(start, end); const getActiveOverride = (dateStr) => { const matching = (schedOverrides || []).filter(s => dateStr >= s.start_date && dateStr <= s.end_date); return matching.find(s => String(s.emp_id) === String(currentUser.emp_id)) || matching.find(s => String(s.emp_id || '').toUpperCase() === 'ALL') || null; }; const isHolidayForCurrentEmployee = (dateStr) => cachedHolidays.some(h => { if (h.date !== dateStr) return false; const coverage = String(h.coverage || 'NATIONWIDE').toUpperCase(); const station = String(currentUser.station || '').toUpperCase().trim(); if (coverage.includes('NATIONWIDE')) return true; const hasNaga = coverage.includes('NAGA CITY'); const hasCamSur = coverage.includes('CAMARINES SUR'); if (hasNaga && hasCamSur) return true; if (hasNaga && !hasCamSur) return station.includes('DARPO') || station === 'DARPO-CS1'; if (hasCamSur && !hasNaga) return station.includes('DARMO') || station !== 'DARPO-CS1'; return false; }); const chargeable = []; let cur = new Date(start + 'T00:00:00'); const eDate = new Date(end + 'T00:00:00'); while (cur <= eDate) { const dateStr = `${cur.getFullYear()}-${String(cur.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(cur.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`; const day = cur.getDay(); const override = getActiveOverride(dateStr); const isCWW = window.isCtoCompressedWorkWeek(override); const isRestDay = day === 0 || day === 6 || (isCWW && day === 5); const isHoliday = isHolidayForCurrentEmployee(dateStr); if (!isRestDay && !isHoliday) { const hours = window.getCtoScheduleHours(override); chargeable.push({ date: dateStr, hours: hours > 0 ? hours : 8 }); } cur.setDate(cur.getDate() + 1); } // CTO half-day is hourly, not "0.5 CTO day". // Regular 8-hour schedule = 4 CTO hours; CWW 10-hour schedule = 5 CTO hours. const halfStart = document.getElementById('leave-half-day-start')?.checked; const halfEnd = document.getElementById('leave-half-day-end')?.checked; if (chargeable.length > 0 && halfStart) chargeable[0].hours /= 2; if (chargeable.length > 0 && halfEnd) chargeable[chargeable.length - 1].hours /= 2; const totalHours = chargeable.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.hours, 0); return { totalHours, dates: chargeable }; }; // EXACT CURRENT CTO BALANCE: // employees.cto_balance (opening balance, already in HOURS) // + all credited cto_records EARNED (PROCESSED; legacy POSTED also honored) // - cto_records USED / CTO DEDUCTION. window.getCurrentCtoHours = async function(empId) { const [{data: empRow, error: empErr}, {data: ctoRows, error: ctoErr}] = await Promise.all([ supabase.from('employees').select('emp_id,cto_balance').eq('emp_id',empId).maybeSingle(), supabase.from('cto_records').select('*').eq('emp_id',empId) ]); if(empErr) throw empErr; if(ctoErr) throw ctoErr; return CTOFEFO.snapshot(empRow || {emp_id:empId,cto_balance:0},ctoRows||[]).availableHours; }; // Available-for-new-application CTO also reserves already-pending CTO leave // applications, so the same hours cannot be committed twice. window.getAvailableCtoHours = async function(empId, excludeLeaveId = null) { let availableHours = await window.getCurrentCtoHours(empId); const { data: pendingRows, error: pendingErr } = await supabase .from('leave_ledger') .select('id, debit, remarks') .eq('emp_id', empId) .eq('transaction_type', 'APPLICATION'); if (pendingErr) throw pendingErr; (pendingRows || []).forEach(l => { if (excludeLeaveId !== null && String(l.id) === String(excludeLeaveId)) return; const lr = String(l.remarks || '').toUpperCase(); if (lr.includes('CREDIT TO CTO') || lr.includes('CREDITED TO CTO')) { availableHours -= window.extractCtoHoursFromRemarks(l.remarks, l.debit); } }); return Math.max(0, availableHours); }; // Display both legacy PSO numbers (e.g., "123") and new typed references // (e.g., "RSO 123") without duplicating the order prefix. window.formatCtoOrderReference = function(value) { const ref = String(value ?? '').trim(); if (!ref) return 'PSO ---'; return /^(?:PSO|RSO|SO)\b/i.test(ref) ? ref : `PSO ${ref}`; }; // EMPLOYEE CTO CREDIT APPLICATION // This employee portal creates the request only. // HR approval/finalization happens in the dedicated Gate Pass & CTO Processing page. let ctoRequestSource = 'credits'; window.openCtoCreditRequest = function(source = 'credits') { const modal = document.getElementById('ctoCreditModal'); if (!modal) return; ctoRequestSource = source === 'leave' ? 'leave' : 'credits'; document.getElementById('cto-credit-order-type').value = 'PSO'; document.getElementById('cto-credit-pso').value = ''; document.getElementById('cto-credit-desc').value = ''; document.getElementById('cto-credit-date').value = ''; const dateEl = document.getElementById('cto-credit-date'); if (dateEl) dateEl.max = getPSTDate(); const titleEl = document.getElementById('cto-credit-modal-title'); const helpEl = document.getElementById('cto-credit-modal-help'); const btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-cto-credit'); if (ctoRequestSource === 'leave') { if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = 'Apply for CTO Credits'; if (helpEl) helpEl.innerHTML = 'Select the <strong>Order Type (PSO, RSO, or SO)</strong> and enter its <strong>Number, Description, and Date</strong>. The application will be sent to HR and will remain pending until processed in the CTO Processing page.'; if (btn) btn.innerText = 'APPLY'; } else { if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = 'Submit CTO Credit to HR'; if (helpEl) helpEl.innerHTML = 'Submit the <strong>Order Type (PSO, RSO, or SO), Number, Description, and Date</strong> for HR verification. This is the same CTO credit application processed in the CTO Processing page.'; if (btn) btn.innerText = 'SUBMIT CTO CREDIT TO HR'; } modal.style.display = 'flex'; }; window.closeCtoCreditRequest = function() { const modal = document.getElementById('ctoCreditModal'); if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none'; }; window.submitCtoCreditRequest = async function() { const orderType = document.getElementById('cto-credit-order-type').value; // Keep the database column PSO_Number for compatibility; store the full reference. // Accept either "123" or a manually typed "PSO 123" in the number field. const orderNumber = document.getElementById('cto-credit-pso').value.trim() .replace(/^(?:PSO|RSO|SO)\s*[#:\-]?\s*/i, '').trim(); const pso = orderNumber ? `${orderType} ${orderNumber}` : ''; const desc = document.getElementById('cto-credit-desc').value.trim(); const activityDate = document.getElementById('cto-credit-date').value; const btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-cto-credit'); if (!pso || !desc || !activityDate) { return alert('Please select an order type and enter the Order Number, Description, and Date of Activity.'); } if (activityDate > getPSTDate()) { return alert('Date of Activity cannot be a future date.'); } try { if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.innerText = 'SUBMITTING...'; } // Prevent active/final duplicates caused by repeat taps or slow connections. // A previously disapproved/cancelled request may be corrected and re-applied. const { data: existingRows, error: dupErr } = await supabase .from('cto_records') .select('id, transaction_type, remarks') .eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id) // Also recognize old PSO records saved as just "123". .in('PSO_Number', orderType === 'PSO' ? [pso, orderNumber] : [pso]) .eq('date', activityDate); if (dupErr) throw dupErr; const activeDuplicate = (existingRows || []).find(r => { const tx = String(r.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const rm = String(r.remarks || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const denied = ['DISAPPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'CANCELLED'].includes(tx) || rm.startsWith('DISAPPROVED') || rm.startsWith('REJECTED') || rm.startsWith('CANCELLED'); return !denied; }); if (activeDuplicate) { return alert(`A CTO application for ${pso} on ${activityDate} already exists (${activeDuplicate.remarks || activeDuplicate.transaction_type || 'recorded'}).`); } // Match the dedicated CTO Processing page: // APPLICATION + FOR HR PROCESSING = pending employee application. // The employee portal intentionally does NOT compute approved hours or multiplier. const { error } = await supabase.from('cto_records').insert({ emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, date: activityDate, transaction_type: 'APPLICATION', base_type: desc, base_hours: '0', multiplier: '1.0', calculated_hours: '0', PSO_Number: pso, remarks: 'FOR HR PROCESSING' }); if (error) throw error; alert('CTO credit application submitted for HR approval. It is not yet added to your CTO balance. HR will process it in the CTO Processing page.'); window.closeCtoCreditRequest(); if (document.getElementById('credits-view')?.classList.contains('active')) { await window.loadCredits(); } } catch (e) { alert('Unable to submit CTO credit application: ' + e.message); } finally { if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerText = ctoRequestSource === 'leave' ? 'APPLY' : 'SUBMIT CTO CREDIT TO HR'; } } }; window.updateLeaveInputs = function() { // Every leave type has its own schedule-aware charging rule. const type = document.querySelector('input[name="ltype"]:checked').value; const container = document.getElementById('leave-details-container'); container.innerHTML = ''; // SPL/WL have whole-day entitlements. Submit a separate leave for each calendar year.</b>'; const btn=document.getElementById('btn-submit-leave'); if(btn)btn.disabled=true; return; } const schedOverrides = await window.fetchLeaveScheduleOverrides(start, end); const getActiveOverride = (dateStr) => { const matching = (schedOverrides || []).filter(s => dateStr >= s.start_date && dateStr <= s.end_date); return matching.find(s => String(s.emp_id) === String(currentUser.emp_id)) || matching.find(s => String(s.emp_id || '').toUpperCase() === 'ALL') || null; }; let count = 0; let excludedWeekends = 0; let excludedHolidays = 0; let excludedFridays = 0; let cur = new Date(sDate); while (cur <= eDate) { const dateStr = `${cur.getFullYear()}-${String(cur.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(cur.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`; const day = cur.getDay(); const isWeekend = (day === 0 || day === 6); const isHoliday = cachedHolidays.some(h => { if (h.date !== dateStr) return false; const coverage = String(h.coverage || 'NATIONWIDE').toUpperCase(); const station = String(currentUser.station || '').toUpperCase().trim(); if (coverage.includes('NATIONWIDE')) return true; const hasNaga = coverage.includes('NAGA CITY'); const hasCamSur = coverage.includes('CAMARINES SUR'); if (hasNaga && hasCamSur) return true; if (hasNaga && !hasCamSur) return station.includes('DARPO') || station === 'DARPO-CS1'; if (hasCamSur && !hasNaga) return station.includes('DARMO') || station !== 'DARPO-CS1'; return false; }); const activeOverride = getActiveOverride(dateStr); const startTime = String(activeOverride?.start_time || '').substring(0, 5); const endTime = String(activeOverride?.end_time || '').substring(0, 5); const isCWW = activeOverride && String(activeOverride.schedule_type || '').toUpperCase() === 'FIXED' && startTime === '07:00' && endTime === '18:00'; const isCWWFriday = isCWW && day === 5; if (isHoliday && !isWeekend) { excludedHolidays++; } else if (isWeekend) { excludedWeekends++; } else if (isCWWFriday) { excludedFridays++; } else { let dayDeduction = 1.0; if (isCWW) { const lTypeElement = document.querySelector('input[name="ltype"]:checked'); const lType = lTypeElement ? lTypeElement.value : ''; if (['Vacation Leave','Sick Leave','Mandatory/Forced Leave'].includes(lType)) dayDeduction = 1.25; } count += dayDeduction; } cur.setDate(cur.getDate() + 1); } const halfStartCheck = document.getElementById('leave-half-day-start'); const halfEndCheck = document.getElementById('leave-half-day-end'); const startOverride = getActiveOverride(start); const startOverrideTime = String(startOverride?.start_time || '').substring(0, 5); const endOverrideTime = String(startOverride?.end_time || '').substring(0, 5); const isCWWActive = startOverride && String(startOverride.schedule_type || '').toUpperCase() === 'FIXED' && startOverrideTime === '07:00' && endOverrideTime === '18:00'; const lTypeElement = document.querySelector('input[name="ltype"]:checked'); const lType = lTypeElement ? lTypeElement.value : ''; const ctoHalfDayCheck = document.getElementById('vl-cto-credit'); const isHourlyCtoLeave = lType === 'Vacation Leave' && !!(ctoHalfDayCheck && ctoHalfDayCheck.checked); let halfDayValue = 0.5; let minAllowed = 1.5; if (isCWWActive && ['Vacation Leave','Sick Leave','Mandatory/Forced Leave'].includes(lType)) { halfDayValue = 0.625; minAllowed = 1.875; } let deduction = 0; if (halfStartCheck && halfStartCheck.checked) deduction += halfDayValue; if (halfEndCheck && halfEndCheck.checked) deduction += halfDayValue; if (deduction > 0) { // Ordinary leave keeps the existing minimum rule. CTO-funded Vacation Leave // is hourly, so a single half-day is allowed and uses schedule_override hours. if (!isHourlyCtoLeave && (count - deduction) < minAllowed) { alert('No half day leave is allowed unless the total leave is at least 1.5 days.'); if (halfStartCheck) halfStartCheck.checked = false; if (halfEndCheck) halfEndCheck.checked = false; deduction = 0; } else if ((count - deduction) <= 0) { alert('Invalid selection. Total leave days cannot be 0.'); if (halfStartCheck) halfStartCheck.checked = false; if (halfEndCheck) halfEndCheck.checked = false; deduction = 0; } else { count -= deduction; } } countEl.innerText = count; if (breakdownEl) { let infoArr = []; if (excludedWeekends > 0) infoArr.push(`<b>${excludedWeekends}</b> Weekend(s)`); if (excludedHolidays > 0) infoArr.push(`<b>${excludedHolidays}</b> Holiday(s)`); if (excludedFridays > 0) infoArr.push(`<b>${excludedFridays}</b> CWW Friday(s)`); if (deduction > 0 && count > 0) infoArr.push(`<b>${deduction}</b> Half-day deduction(s)`); breakdownEl.innerHTML = infoArr.length > 0 ? `Automatically excluded: ${infoArr.join(', ')}` : 'All selected dates are standard working days.'; } const ctoCheck = document.getElementById('vl-cto-credit'); const isCtoCredited = ctoCheck && ctoCheck.checked; const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-leave'); if (count > 0 && isCtoCredited) { try { const availableCtoHours = await window.getAvailableCtoHours(currentUser.emp_id); const ctoSchedule = await window.getCtoRequiredHours(start, end); const requiredCtoHours = ctoSchedule.totalHours; if (requiredCtoHours <= 0) { if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = true; if (breakdownEl) breakdownEl.innerHTML += '<br><b style="color:var(--danger);">No chargeable CTO work hours were found for the selected dates.</b>'; return; } const ctoHoursToUse = Math.min(Math.max(availableCtoHours, 0), requiredCtoHours); const remainingHours = Math.max(0, requiredCtoHours - ctoHoursToUse); const vlFallbackDays = remainingHours / 8; if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = false; if (remainingHours > 0.0001) { countEl.innerText = `${count} (CTO: ${ctoHoursToUse.toFixed(2)} HRS + VL: ${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)} DAY/S)`; if (breakdownEl) { breakdownEl.innerHTML += `<br><b>CTO available for this application:</b> ${availableCtoHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s).` + `<br><b>Required:</b> ${requiredCtoHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s).` + `<br><b>Proposed fallback:</b> ${ctoHoursToUse.toFixed(2)} CTO hour(s) + ${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)} Vacation Leave day(s). You will be asked to confirm before submission.` + `<br><button type="button" onclick="window.openCtoCreditRequest('leave')" style="margin-top:6px; border:1px solid var(--dar-gold); background:#fefce8; color:#854d0e; border-radius:6px; padding:6px 9px; font-size:10px; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;">APPLY FOR CTO CREDITS</button>`; } } else { countEl.innerText = `${count} (CTO: ${requiredCtoHours.toFixed(2)} HRS)`; if (breakdownEl) { breakdownEl.innerHTML += `<br><b>CTO charge:</b> ${requiredCtoHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s), based on the applicable work schedule.`; } } } catch (e) { console.error('CTO balance/schedule check failed:', e); if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = true; countEl.innerText = count; if (breakdownEl) breakdownEl.innerHTML += `<br><b style="color:var(--danger);">Unable to verify CTO balance: ${String(e.message || e)}</b>`; } } else { if (submitBtn) submitBtn.disabled = false; countEl.innerText = count; } }; window.submitLeave = async function() { if (isSubmitting) return; const type = document.querySelector('input[name="ltype"]:checked').value; const start = document.getElementById('leave-from').value; const end = document.getElementById('leave-to').value; const days = parseFloat(document.getElementById('leave-days-count').innerText); const comm = document.getElementById('leave-commutation').value; const ctoCheck = document.getElementById('vl-cto-credit'); const isCtoCredited = ctoCheck && ctoCheck.checked; if (!start || !end || days <= 0) return alert("Select valid working dates."); if (['Special Privilege Leave','Wellness Leave'].includes(type) && start.slice(0,4)!==end.slice(0,4)) return alert('SPL and Wellness Leave must be filed separately for each calendar year.'); if (type === "Vacation Leave" && !isCtoCredited) { const filingDate = new Date(todayStr); const leaveStart = new Date(start); const diffTime = leaveStart - filingDate; const diffDaysRule = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)); if (diffDaysRule < 5) return alert("VACATION LEAVE must be filed at least 5 days before availment."); } // Shared exact ledger/baseline calculation used by both portals. const {data:empData,error:escEmployeeError}=await supabase.from('employees') .select('vl_balance,sl_balance,spl_balance,wl_balance,election_service_credit_balance') .eq('emp_id',currentUser.emp_id).maybeSingle(); if(escEmployeeError || !empData)return alert('Unable to verify leave balances: '+(escEmployeeError?.message||'Employee not found')); const allLedger=await LeaveParity.fetchAll(supabase,c=>c.from('leave_ledger') .select('id,emp_id,type,credit,debit,transaction_type,remarks,start_date,created_at') .eq('emp_id',currentUser.emp_id).order('id',{ascending:true})); // SPL/WL are charged against the year OF LEAVE, not the year filed. let splWlFallbackApproved=false; const leaveYear=Number(start.slice(0,4)); const balanceNow=LeaveParity.balance({...empData,emp_id:currentUser.emp_id},allLedger,leaveYear); let currentVL=balanceNow.VL, currentSL=balanceNow.SL, currentSPL=balanceNow.SPL,currentWL=balanceNow.WL,currentESC=balanceNow.ESC; // Pending SPL/WL requests in the same year reserve days only while still active. // An APPROVED transaction is already counted in balanceNow and is not reserved twice. const reservedDays=allLedger.reduce((sum,r)=>{ if(Number(String(r.start_date||'').slice(0,4))!==leaveYear) return sum; if(LeaveParity.category(r.type)!==(type==='Special Privilege Leave'?'SPL':type==='Wellness Leave'?'WL':'NONE')) return sum; if(!/^(APPLICATION|PENDING|SUBMITTED|REQUESTED)/i.test(String(r.transaction_type||'')))return sum; return sum+Math.max(0,Number(r.debit)||0); },0); currentSPL=Math.max(0,currentSPL-(type==='Special Privilege Leave'?reservedDays:0)); currentWL=Math.max(0,currentWL-(type==='Wellness Leave'?reservedDays:0)); if (type === "Vacation Leave" || type === "Mandatory/Forced Leave") { if (!(type === "Vacation Leave" && isCtoCredited) && days > currentVL) return alert(`Insufficient Vacation Leave balance. You only have ${currentVL.toFixed(3)} days available.`); } else if (type === "Special Privilege Leave" || type === "Wellness Leave") { // One day is one SPL/WL credit even on CWW. Uncovered scheduled // days may use VL only with express employee confirmation. const creditsAvailable=type==='Special Privilege Leave' ? currentSPL : currentWL; const schedule=await window.getCtoRequiredHours(start,end); const daysInSchedule=schedule.dates||[]; if(Math.abs(daysInSchedule.length-days)>0.0005) return alert('The leave schedule changed. Recalculate the leave dates before submitting.'); const coveredDays=Math.min(days,Math.max(0,Math.floor(creditsAvailable+0.00001))); const uncovered=daysInSchedule.slice(coveredDays); const vlEquivalent=uncovered.reduce((n,d)=>n+Number(d.hours||8)/8,0); if(uncovered.length) { if(vlEquivalent>currentVL+0.0005) return alert(`Insufficient leave credits. ${coveredDays} ${type==='Special Privilege Leave'?'SPL':'WL'} day(s) available; ${uncovered.length} uncovered day(s) would cost ${vlEquivalent.toFixed(3)} VL credits, but only ${currentVL.toFixed(3)} VL remain.`); if(!confirm(`${type} available for ${leaveYear}: ${coveredDays} day(s).\nCharge the other ${uncovered.length} workday(s) to Vacation Leave at ACTUAL scheduled hours (${vlEquivalent.toFixed(3)} VL credit(s); 10-hour CWW day = 1.25)?`))return; splWlFallbackApproved=true; } } else if (type === "Election Service Credit Leave") { if (days > currentESC + 0.0005) return alert(`Insufficient verified Election Service Credit balance. You only have ${currentESC.toFixed(3)} day(s). Contact HR to post your COMELEC-certified credit. This leave cannot automatically draw from VL.`); } else if (type === "Sick Leave") { if (days > currentSL) { let deficit = days - currentSL; if (deficit > currentVL) { return alert(`Insufficient Sick & Vacation Leave balances. SL: ${currentSL.toFixed(3)}, VL: ${currentVL.toFixed(3)}.`); } else { let proceed = confirm(`Insufficient Sick Leave (${currentSL.toFixed(3)} days). As a last resort, the remaining ${deficit.toFixed(3)} days will be charged to your Vacation Leave. Proceed?`); if (!proceed) return; } } } // CTO CHECK LOGIC. Use CTO first; if it cannot fully cover the leave, // ask the employee before charging the remainder to Vacation Leave. let requiredCtoHours = 0; let ctoHoursToUse = 0; let vlFallbackDays = 0; if (isCtoCredited) { try { const availableCtoHours = await window.getAvailableCtoHours(currentUser.emp_id); const ctoSchedule = await window.getCtoRequiredHours(start, end); requiredCtoHours = ctoSchedule.totalHours; if (requiredCtoHours <= 0) return alert('No chargeable CTO work hours were found for the selected dates.'); ctoHoursToUse = Math.min(Math.max(availableCtoHours, 0), requiredCtoHours); const remainingHours = Math.max(0, requiredCtoHours - ctoHoursToUse); vlFallbackDays = remainingHours / 8; if (remainingHours > 0.0001) { if (vlFallbackDays > currentVL + 0.0001) { alert(`CTO and Vacation Leave are not enough for this application. CTO available: ${availableCtoHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s). Vacation Leave available: ${currentVL.toFixed(3)} day(s). Additional VL needed after CTO: ${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)} day(s).`); if (confirm('Would you like to apply for CTO credits first?')) window.openCtoCreditRequest('leave'); return; } const splitMessage = ctoHoursToUse > 0.0001 ? `Your CTO balance can cover ${ctoHoursToUse.toFixed(2)} of ${requiredCtoHours.toFixed(2)} required hour(s). Charge the remaining ${remainingHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s) (${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)} day/s) to Vacation Leave?` : `You currently have no available CTO credit for this application. Charge the required ${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)} day(s) to Vacation Leave instead?`; if (!confirm(splitMessage)) { if (confirm('Submit CTO credit to HR for validation instead? You can apply for CTO leave after HR processes the credit.')) { window.openCtoCreditRequest('leave'); } return; } } } catch (e) { console.error('CTO balance/schedule check failed:', e); return alert('Unable to verify CTO balance: ' + (e.message || e)); } } const rDetail = document.querySelector('input[name="ldetail"]:checked'); const specify = document.getElementById('leave-specify'); let detailStr = rDetail ? rDetail.value : "See Remarks"; if (isCtoCredited && ctoHoursToUse > 0.0001) { detailStr += ` | CREDIT TO CTO | CTO HOURS: ${ctoHoursToUse.toFixed(2)}`; if (vlFallbackDays > 0.0001) detailStr += ` | VL CHARGE: ${vlFallbackDays.toFixed(3)}`; } else if (isCtoCredited && vlFallbackDays > 0.0001) { detailStr += ` | CTO UNAVAILABLE - CHARGED TO VACATION LEAVE`; } if (specify && specify.value.trim() !== "") detailStr += ` - ${specify.value.trim()}`; // LEAVE APPROVAL ROUTING (Initial Assignment) let routeStatus = "PENDING_CHIEF"; const empUnitStr = currentUser.unit ? String(currentUser.unit).toUpperCase().trim() : ""; const isMARPO = currentUser.position && String(currentUser.position).toUpperCase().includes("MARPO"); if (empUnitStr === "PARPO") { routeStatus = "PENDING_PARPO"; } else if (empUnitStr.includes("DARMO")) { routeStatus = isMARPO ? "PENDING_CHIEF" : "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD"; } else if (empUnitStr !== "" && empUnitStr !== "NA") { routeStatus = "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD"; } else { routeStatus = "PENDING_CHIEF"; } const fullRemarks = `${detailStr} | Commutation: ${comm} | ROUTING_STATUS: ${routeStatus}` + (splWlFallbackApproved ? ' | SPL_WL_VL_FALLBACK_CONSENT: YES' : ''); const dates_to_check = []; let curr = new Date(start); const eDate = new Date(end); while (curr <= eDate) { dates_to_check.push(curr.toISOString().split('T')[0]); curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1); } const activeToReq = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('to_no, status, transport, unit, emp_name').eq('emp_id', currentUser.emp_id).not('status', 'ilike', '%CANCELLED%'); const { data: toConflicts } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('to_no, travel_date') .gte('travel_date', start).lte('travel_date', end) .in('to_no', (activeToReq.data || []).map(t => t.to_no)); if (toConflicts && toConflicts.length > 0) { const uniqueToNos = [...new Set(toConflicts.map(t => t.to_no))]; for (let to_no of uniqueToNos) { const toData = activeToReq.data.find(t => t.to_no === to_no); const { data: fullItin } = await supabase.from('itinerary').select('id, travel_date').eq('to_no', to_no); if (fullItin.length === 1) { alert(`Conflict: You have a 1-day Travel Order (${to_no}) on ${fullItin[0].travel_date}. You must cancel this TO first before applying for leave.`); return; } else { const conflictingDates = toConflicts.filter(c => c.to_no === to_no).map(c => c.travel_date); if (!confirm(`Conflict: You have a multi-day TO (${to_no}). Do you want to CANCEL the specific days (${conflictingDates.join(', ')}) that conflict with this Leave?`)) { return; } await supabase.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', to_no).in('travel_date', conflictingDates); if (toData.status === 'APPROVED' || toData.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT') { await supabase.from('travel_orders').update({status: 'CANCELLED'}).eq('to_no', to_no); await window.logActivity(to_no, 'CANCELLED', 'Original TO cancelled due to Leave conflict override'); const newToNo = await window.generateToNo(currentUser.division ? currentUser.division.split(',')[0].trim() : "GEN"); const { data: origToData } = await supabase.from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', to_no).maybeSingle(); if (origToData) { const { id, created_at, ...restTo } = origToData; await supabase.from('travel_orders').insert({ ...restTo, to_no: newToNo, status: toData.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' ? 'READY_FOR_PRINT' : 'APPROVED' }); await supabase.from('itinerary').update({ to_no: newToNo }).eq('to_no', to_no); await window.logActivity(newToNo, 'SPLIT_CREATED', 'Generated from Leave conflict override'); } } else { let routeStatus = "PENDING_CHIEF"; const isGovVeh = toData.transport === "GOV'T VEHICLE"; const isPARPOUnit = toData.unit && String(toData.unit).toUpperCase() === "PARPO"; if (isGovVeh) { routeStatus = "PENDING_VEHICLE_APPROVER"; } else if (isPARPOUnit) { routeStatus = "PENDING_PARPO"; } else if (toData.unit && toData.unit.trim() !== "" && toData.unit !== "NA") { routeStatus = "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD"; } await supabase.from('travel_orders').update({ status: routeStatus, unithead_signed: null, dc_signed: null, parpo_signed: null }).eq('to_no', to_no); await window.logActivity(to_no, 'MODIFIED_ROUTED_BACK', 'Pending TO overridden and routed back due to Leave conflict'); } } } } isSubmitting = true; const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-leave'); if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = true; submitBtn.innerText = "Processing..."; } try { const { error: insertErr } = await supabase.from('leave_ledger').insert({ transaction_id: crypto.randomUUID(), emp_id: currentUser.emp_id, start_date: start, end_date: end, type: type, debit: days, transaction_type: 'APPLICATION', remarks: fullRemarks }); if (insertErr) throw new Error("Leave insert failed: " + insertErr.message); alert("Leave application submitted for routing. function(tab) { currentTab = tab; selectedPendingOrders.clear(); renderMain(); } window.switchLeaveSubTab = function(subTab) { currentLeaveSubTab = subTab; selectedPendingOrders.clear(); loadLeavesTab(); } window.showChangePassword = function() { document.getElementById('pwdModal').style.display = 'flex'; } window.hideChangePassword = function() { document.getElementById('pwdModal').style.display = 'none'; } async function updatePassword() { const o = document.getElementById('oldPwd').value.trim(); const n = document.getElementById('newPwd').value.trim(); const { data } = await supabaseClient.from('users').select('password').eq('username', currentUser.username).single(); const hashedOld = await hashPassword(o); if (data.password !== o && data.password !== hashedOld) { return alert("Incorrect."); } const hashedNew = await hashPassword(n); await supabaseClient.from('users').update({ password: hashedNew }).eq('username', currentUser.username); alert("Updated!"); hideChangePassword(); } // --- CANCEL MODAL LOGIC (Approved Tab) --- window.showCancelModal = function(to_no, datesEncoded) { focus:ring-red-500 border-gray-300"> <span class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">${d}</span> </label> `; }); document.getElementById('cancelModal').style.display = 'flex'; } window.hideCancelModal = function() { document.getElementById('cancelModal').style.display = 'none'; } window.cancelEntireTO = async function() { if(confirm("Are you sure you want to cancel this ENTIRE Travel Order?")) { ${currentUser.fullName}`); await supabaseClient.from('notifications').insert({ recipient_role: 'PARPO', message: `Approved TO ${targetToNo} had dates cancelled (${selectedDates.join(', ')}) by ${currentUser.fullName}`, to_no: targetToNo }); loadStatusTab('APPROVED'); } } async function processFullCancel(to_no) { const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); content.innerHTML = `<p TO ${to_no} was entirely cancelled by ${currentUser.fullName}`, to_no: to_no }); loadStatusTab('APPROVED'); } // --- SHARED SCOPE CHECK --- const checkScope = (div, unit) => { const roles = currentUser.roles || []; // ADDED: "PARPO_I", "PARPO I", "PARPO1" to provincial-level approvers if (roles.some(r => ["PARPO", "PARPO_II", "PARPO II", "PARPO_I", "PARPO I", "PARPO1", "VEHICLE_APPROVER", "VEHICLE APPROVER", "ADMIN", "HRMO"].includes(r))) return true; const oDivs = div ? div.split(',').map(x => x.trim().toUpperCase()) : []; const dMatch = oDivs.some(d => currentUser.divisions.includes(d)); if (roles.includes("CHIEF")) return dMatch; if (roles.some(r => ["UNIT_HEAD", "UNIT HEAD"].includes(r)) && div && div.toUpperCase().includes("PBDD") && dMatch) return true; const oUnits = unit ? unit.split(',').map(x => x.trim().toUpperCase()) : []; const uMatch = oUnits.some(u => currentUser.units.includes(u)); return dMatch && uMatch; }; // --- BATCH ACTION LOGIC --- window.toggleBatchSelection = function(id, isChecked) { if (isChecked) selectedPendingOrders.add(id); else selectedPendingOrders.delete(id); updateBatchActionBar(); } window.selectAllPending = function(checkbox) { const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.to-checkbox'); checkboxes.forEach(cb => { cb.checked = checkbox.checked; if (checkbox.checked) selectedPendingOrders.add(cb.value); else selectedPendingOrders.delete(cb.value); }); updateBatchActionBar(); } function updateBatchActionBar() { const bar = document.getElementById('batchActionBar'); const countText = document.getElementById('batchCountText'); if (selectedPendingOrders.size > 0) { countText.innerText = `${selectedPendingOrders.size} selected`; bar.classList.remove('translate-y-full'); } else { hideBatchActionBar(); } } function hideBatchActionBar() { const bar = document.getElementById('batchActionBar'); if(bar) bar.classList.add('translate-y-full'); } window.processBatch = async function(action) { try { if (selectedPendingOrders.size === 0) return; hideBatchActionBar(); let denyReason = ""; if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { denyReason = prompt("Reason for denying ALL selected:"); if (!denyReason) { if (currentTab === 'leaves') loadLeavesTab(); else loadPendingTab(); return; } } const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); content.innerHTML = `<p class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-dar-green font-bold animate-pulse">Processing Batch...</p>`; const toNos = Array.from(selectedPendingOrders).filter(id => id.startsWith('TO:')).map(id => id.split(':')[1]); const leaveIds = Array.from(selectedPendingOrders).filter(id => id.startsWith('LEAVE:')).map(id => id.split(':')[1]); // Each TO has one travel_orders row per traveler. Preserve each person's approval stage. if (toNos.length > 0) { for (const to_no of toNos) { if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { const { error } = await supabaseClient.from('travel_orders') .update({ status: "DISAPPROVED", received: denyReason }).eq('to_no', to_no); if (error) throw error; await window.logActivity(to_no, 'DISAPPROVED', `Batch disapproved by ${currentUser.fullName}. Reason: ${denyReason}`); } else { await approveGroupTOStage(to_no); } } } selectedPendingOrders.clear(); loadPendingTab(); } catch (err) { alert("Batch processing error: " + err.message); loadPendingTab(); } } // --- PENDING MODAL & DIRECT ACTIONS --- window.hidePendingDateModal = function() { document.getElementById('pendingDateModal').style.display = 'none'; }; window.initiateDirectTO = function(to_no, action, itinEncoded) { const itinerary = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(itinEncoded)); currentPendingToNo = to_no; currentPendingAction = action; const destLabel = document.getElementById('pendingModalDestination'); if(destLabel) destLabel.style.display = 'none'; const list = document.getElementById('pendingDatesList'); list.innerHTML = ''; itinerary.forEach(i => { const dStr = new Date(i.travel_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'}); list.innerHTML += ` <label class="flex items-center space-x-3 p-3 border rounded-xl bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer"> <input type="checkbox" value="${i.travel_date}" class="pending-date-cb w-5 h-5 rounded focus:ring-green-500 border-gray-300" checked> <div class="flex flex-col"> <span class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">${dStr}</span> <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">📍 ${i.destination || 'N/A'}</span> </div> </label> `; }); const reasonInput = document.getElementById('pendingDenyReason'); const approveInput = document.getElementById('pendingApproveReason'); const partialDenyInput = document.getElementById('pendingPartialDenyReason'); const hdr = document.getElementById('pendingDateModalHeader'); const btn = document.getElementById('pendingDateConfirmBtn'); const txt = document.getElementById('pendingDateModalText'); partialDenyInput.classList.add('hidden'); partialDenyInput.value = ''; if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { hdr.className = "bg-red-600 text-white p-4 font-bold text-center relative"; hdr.innerHTML = `Deny Dates <button onclick="hidePendingDateModal()" class="absolute right-4 text-xl leading-none">✕</button>`; btn.className = "w-full bg-red-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl mt-2"; txt.innerText = "Select dates to DENY (Unselected dates remain pending):"; reasonInput.classList.remove('hidden'); approveInput.classList.add('hidden'); } else { hdr.className = "dar-green text-white p-4 font-bold text-center relative"; hdr.innerHTML = `Approve Dates <button onclick="hidePendingDateModal()" class="absolute right-4 text-xl leading-none">✕</button>`; btn.className = "w-full dar-green text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl mt-2"; txt.innerText = "Select dates to Approve (Unselected dates will be removed):"; reasonInput.classList.add('hidden'); const checkApproveReasonVisibility = () => { const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.pending-date-cb'); const checkedCount = Array.from(checkboxes).filter(cb => cb.checked).length; const totalCount = checkboxes.length; if (checkedCount === 1) { approveInput.classList.remove('hidden'); } else { approveInput.classList.add('hidden'); } if (checkedCount < totalCount) { partialDenyInput.classList.remove('hidden'); } else { partialDenyInput.classList.add('hidden'); } }; const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.pending-date-cb'); checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.addEventListener('change', checkApproveReasonVisibility)); checkApproveReasonVisibility(); } document.getElementById('pendingDateConfirmBtn').onclick = processPendingDates; document.getElementById('pendingDateModal').style.display = 'flex'; }; window.processPendingDates = async function() { const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.pending-date-cb'); const selectedDates = Array.from(checkboxes).filter(cb => cb.checked).map(cb => cb.value); const totalDates = checkboxes.length; if (selectedDates.length === 0) return alert("Select at least one date."); let denyReason = ""; let approveReason = ""; let partialDenyReason = ""; if (currentPendingAction === 'DISAPPROVED') { denyReason = document.getElementById('pendingDenyReason').value.trim(); if (!denyReason && selectedDates.length === totalDates) return alert("Please provide a reason for denying the entire TO."); } else { if (selectedDates.length === 1) { approveReason = document.getElementById('pendingApproveReason').value.trim(); } if (selectedDates.length < totalDates) { partialDenyReason = document.getElementById('pendingPartialDenyReason').value.trim(); if (!partialDenyReason) return alert("Please provide a reason for the unchecked (disapproved) dates."); } } hidePendingDateModal(); await executePartialTO(currentPendingToNo, currentPendingAction, selectedDates, totalDates, denyReason, approveReason, partialDenyReason); }; window.executePartialTO = async function(to_no, action, selectedDates, totalDates, denyReason, approveReason, partialDenyReason) { const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); content.innerHTML = `<p class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-dar-green font-bold animate-pulse">Processing...</p>`; try { if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { if (selectedDates.length === totalDates) { await supabaseClient.from('travel_orders').update({ status: 'DISAPPROVED', received: denyReason }).eq('to_no', to_no); await window.logActivity(to_no, 'DISAPPROVED', `Disapproved by ${currentUser.fullName}. Reason: ${denyReason}`); } else { for (let d of selectedDates) { await supabaseClient.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', to_no).eq('travel_date', d); } await window.logActivity(to_no, 'PARTIAL_DENY', `Dates denied and removed (${selectedDates.join(', ')}) by ${currentUser.fullName}`); } } else { if (selectedDates.length < totalDates) { const unselectedDates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.pending-date-cb')).filter(cb => !cb.checked).map(cb => cb.value); for (let d of unselectedDates) { await supabaseClient.from('itinerary').delete().eq('to_no', to_no).eq('travel_date', d); } await window.logActivity(to_no, 'PARTIAL_DENY', `Dates denied and removed (${unselectedDates.join(', ')}) by ${currentUser.fullName}. Reason: ${partialDenyReason}`); } // Approve only the group members currently at this approver's stage. // This includes a traveling Division Chief at the recommending-approval step; // the PARPO remains responsible for final approval. await approveGroupTOStage(to_no, approveReason); if (approveReason) { await supabaseClient.from('notifications').insert({ recipient_role: 'EMPLOYEE', message: `Your TO ${to_no} was processed. Reason/Note: ${approveReason}`, to_no: to_no }); } } loadPendingTab(); } catch(e) { alert(e.message); loadPendingTab(); } }; function getAppPayload(u, toRecord = null) { const nowISO = new Date().toISOString(); const roles = u.roles || []; const currentStatus = toRecord ? toRecord.status : ""; if (currentStatus === "PENDING_VEHICLE_APPROVER" && roles.some(r => ["VEHICLE_APPROVER", "VEHICLE APPROVER", "ADMIN"].includes(r))) { let nextStatus = "PENDING_CHIEF"; if (toRecord && toRecord.unit) { const unt = toRecord.unit.trim().toUpperCase(); if (unt === "PARPO") nextStatus = "PENDING_PARPO"; else if (unt !== "") nextStatus = "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD"; } return { status: nextStatus }; } if (currentStatus === "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD" && roles.some(r => ["UNIT_HEAD", "UNIT HEAD", "MARPO"].includes(r))) { if (!toRecord) return {}; const requiredUnits = toRecord.unit ? toRecord.unit.split(',').map(x => x.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(x => x && x !== 'NA') : []; const matchingUnitsToSign = requiredUnits.filter(reqU => u.units.includes(reqU)); let currentSigs = toRecord.unithead_signed ? toRecord.unithead_signed : ""; matchingUnitsToSign.forEach(unit => { const sigFragment = `${u.fullName} (${unit})`; if (!currentSigs.includes(sigFragment)) { currentSigs = currentSigs ? currentSigs + ", " + sigFragment : sigFragment; } }); let allSigned = true; if (requiredUnits.length > 0) { allSigned = requiredUnits.every(reqU => currentSigs.toUpperCase().includes(`(${reqU})`)); } return { status: allSigned ? "PENDING_CHIEF" : "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD", unithead_signed: currentSigs, uh_signed_time: nowISO }; } // MODIFIED: Intercept Division Chief approval to route ARBDSP orders to PARPO I if (currentStatus === "PENDING_CHIEF" && roles.includes("CHIEF")) { const isARBDSP = toRecord && ( String(toRecord.division || "").toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP") || String(toRecord.unit || "").toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP") || String(toRecord.fund_source || "").toUpperCase().includes("ARBDSP") ); return { status: isARBDSP ? "PENDING_PARPO_I" : "PENDING_PARPO", dc_signed: u.fullName, dc_signed_time: nowISO }; } // ADDED: Handle PARPO I approval step before PARPO II if (currentStatus === "PENDING_PARPO_I" && roles.some(r => ["PARPO_I", "PARPO I", "PARPO1", "PARPO_1", "ADMIN"].includes(r))) { return { status: "PENDING_PARPO", parpo1_signed: u.fullName, parpo1_signed_time: nowISO }; } // MODIFIED: Ensure PARPO II checks cover standard PARPO role names if (currentStatus === "PENDING_PARPO" && roles.some(r => ["PARPO", "PARPO_II", "PARPO II", "PARPO2", "ADMIN"].includes(r))) { return { status: "APPROVED", parpo_signed: u.fullName, parpo_signed_time: nowISO }; } return {}; } // GROUP TO APPROVAL: one TO number, independent approval statuses per employee. // In particular, a Chief who is a traveler may recommend the GROUP TO; // this is not its final authorization (which remains with PARPO). function findGroupTOApproval(actor, groupRows) { const roles = actor.roles || []; const divisions = actor.divisions || []; const units = actor.units || []; const status = row => String(row.status || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const inChiefDivision = row => String(row.division || '').split(',') .some(d => divisions.includes(d.trim().toUpperCase())); const pendingForStage = (stage, predicate) => groupRows.filter( row => status(row) === stage && predicate(row) ); const hasAnyRole = names => roles.some(role => names.includes(role)); if (hasAnyRole(['VEHICLE_APPROVER', 'VEHICLE APPROVER', 'ADMIN'])) { const rows = pendingForStage('PENDING_VEHICLE_APPROVER', () => true); if (rows.length) return { stage: 'PENDING_VEHICLE_APPROVER', rows }; } if (hasAnyRole(['UNIT_HEAD', 'UNIT HEAD', 'MARPO'])) { const rows = pendingForStage('PENDING_UNIT_HEAD', row => { if (!inChiefDivision(row)) return false; const requiredUnits = String(row.unit || '').split(',') .map(unit => unit.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(unit => unit && unit !== 'NA'); const alreadySigned = String(row.unithead_signed || '').toUpperCase(); return requiredUnits.some(unit => units.includes(unit) && !alreadySigned.includes(`(${unit})`)); }); if (rows.length) return { stage: 'PENDING_UNIT_HEAD', rows }; } if (roles.includes('CHIEF')) { const chiefRows = groupRows.filter(inChiefDivision); // Do not advance the Chief's own row ahead of staff awaiting Unit Head review. const unitReviewsComplete = chiefRows.every(row => !['PENDING_UNIT_HEAD', 'PENDING_VEHICLE_APPROVER'].includes(status(row)) ); if (unitReviewsComplete) { const rows = pendingForStage('PENDING_CHIEF', inChiefDivision); if (rows.length) return { stage: 'PENDING_CHIEF', rows }; } } if (hasAnyRole(['PARPO_I', 'PARPO I', 'PARPO1', 'PARPO_1', 'ADMIN'])) { const rows = pendingForStage('PENDING_PARPO_I', () => true); if (rows.length) return { stage: 'PENDING_PARPO_I', rows }; } if (hasAnyRole(['PARPO', 'PARPO_II', 'PARPO II', 'PARPO2', 'ADMIN'])) { // Final approval must not finish a mixed-stage TO prematurely. const allReadyForFinal = groupRows.every(row => ['PENDING_PARPO', 'APPROVED'].includes(status(row)) ); if (allReadyForFinal) { const rows = pendingForStage('PENDING_PARPO', () => true); if (rows.length) return { stage: 'PENDING_PARPO', rows }; } } return null; } async function approveGroupTOStage(to_no, approveReason = '') { const { data: groupRows, error: readError } = await supabaseClient .from('travel_orders').select('*').eq('to_no', to_no); if (readError) throw readError; if (!groupRows || !groupRows.length) throw new Error(`Travel Order ${to_no} not found.`); const approval = findGroupTOApproval(currentUser, groupRows); if (!approval) throw new Error( `TO ${to_no} has no records ready for your approval stage. ` + 'A staff member may still be awaiting Unit Head approval, or this TO was processed by another approver.' ); const chiefIsTraveler = approval.stage === 'PENDING_CHIEF' && groupRows.some(row => String(row.emp_name || '').trim().toUpperCase() === String(currentUser.fullName || '').trim().toUpperCase() ); for (const row of approval.rows) { const payload = getAppPayload(currentUser, row); if (!payload || !Object.keys(payload).length) { throw new Error(`Cannot advance ${row.emp_name || row.emp_id} at ${row.status}.`); } if (approveReason) payload.remarks = approveReason; // Match TO + employee + current stage, not all employees sharing the TO number. const { data: updated, error: updateError } = await supabaseClient .from('travel_orders').update(payload) .eq('to_no', to_no).eq('emp_id', row.emp_id).eq('status', row.status) .select('emp_id'); if (updateError) throw updateError; if (!updated || updated.length !== 1) { throw new Error(`TO ${to_no}: status changed for ${row.emp_name || row.emp_id}; please refresh and retry.`); } } const actionDescription = chiefIsTraveler ? 'Division Chief is a traveler; recommending approval only, subject to final PARPO approval.' : 'Role-based approval step completed.'; await window.logActivity(to_no, 'STATUS_UPDATED', `${approval.rows.length} participant(s) processed at ${approval.stage} by ${currentUser.fullName}. ${actionDescription}` ); return approval; } // --- PENDING TAB (TO ONLY) --- async function loadPendingTab() { selectedPendingOrders.clear(); const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); content.innerHTML = `<p class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-400 font-bold animate-pulse">Syncing Inbox...</p>`; const [oRes, iRes] = await Promise.all([ supabaseClient.from('travel_orders').select('*'), supabaseClient.from('itinerary').select('*') ]); let pendingItems = []; // -- TOs -- let grp = {}; (oRes.data || []).forEach(o => { if (!grp[o.to_no]) grp[o.to_no] = []; grp[o.to_no].push(o); }); for (const [to_no, list] of Object.entries(grp)) { // The inbox and the approval handler must agree on which stage is actionable. // A Chief traveling on this same TO may still recommend it after staff/unit review. const groupApproval = findGroupTOApproval(currentUser, list); if (groupApproval) { let fullItinerary = (iRes.data || []).filter(i => i.to_no === to_no).sort((a,b) => new Date(a.travel_date) - new Date(b.travel_date)); let dt = "N/A"; if(fullItinerary.length > 0) { const start = new Date(fullItinerary[0].travel_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); const end = new Date(fullItinerary[fullItinerary.length-1].travel_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); dt = fullItinerary.length === 1 ? end : `${start} - ${end}`; } const itinEncoded = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(fullItinerary)).replace(/'/g, "%27"); const itinDetailsHTML = fullItinerary.map(i => { const d = new Date(i.travel_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric'}); return `<div class="mt-1.5 bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-100"> <div class="flex items-start"> <span class="font-bold text-[10px] text-dar-green w-12 shrink-0">${d}</span> <div class="flex flex-col"> <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-700 font-bold leading-tight">📍 ${i.destination || 'N/A'}</span> <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5 leading-tight">🎯 ${i.purpose || 'N/A'}</span> </div> </div> </div>`; }).join(''); pendingItems.push({ type: 'TO', id: to_no, names: list.map(g => g.emp_name), itinDetailsHTML: itinDetailsHTML, report_to: list[0].report_to || 'N/A', dateStr: dt, itinEncoded: itinEncoded, approvalStage: groupApproval.stage, chiefIsTraveler: groupApproval.stage === 'PENDING_CHIEF' && list.some(g => String(g.emp_name || '').trim().toUpperCase() === String(currentUser.fullName || '').trim().toUpperCase()), created_at: list[0].created_at }); } } pendingItems.sort((a,b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at)); if (pendingItems.length === 0) content.innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full opacity-50"><span class="text-4xl">✨</span><p class="font-bold mt-2 text-xs uppercase">Inbox Clear</p></div>`; else { let h = ` <div class="sticky top-0 bg-gray-100 z-20 pt-4 px-4 pb-2 flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm border-b"> <label class="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer text-sm font-bold text-gray-700"> <input type="checkbox" class="batch-checkbox" onchange="selectAllPending(this)"> <span>Select All</span> </label> <span class="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold">${pendingItems.length} Pending</span> </div> <div class="p-4 space-y-4 pb-32">`; pendingItems.forEach(o => { h += ` <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border-2 border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/20 flex gap-3 relative mb-4"> <div class="pt-1"> <input type="checkbox" value="TO:${o.id}" class="batch-checkbox to-checkbox" onchange="toggleBatchSelection('TO:${o.id}', this.checked)"> </div> <div class="flex-grow overflow-hidden"> <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2"> <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-white px-2 py-1 rounded shadow-sm tracking-wide uppercase bg-blue-600"> ✈️ TRAVEL ORDER - ${o.id} </span> </div> <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 leading-tight">${o.names.join(', ')}</p> ${o.chiefIsTraveler ? 'border-dar-green' : 'border-red-500'; let textColor = 'text-gray-400'; if (tabStatus === 'APPROVED') { let canSeeApproved = false; // MODIFIED: Added 'PENDING_PARPO_I' to visible tracking statuses if (roles.some(r => ["UNIT_HEAD", "UNIT HEAD", "MARPO"].includes(r)) && ['PENDING_CHIEF', 'PENDING_PARPO_I', 'PENDING_PARPO', 'APPROVED', 'READY_FOR_PRINT'].includes(o.status)) canSeeApproved = true; if (roles.includes("CHIEF") && ['PENDING_PARPO_I', 'PENDING_PARPO', 'APPROVED', 'READY_FOR_PRINT'].includes(o.status)) canSeeApproved = true; if (roles.some(r => ["PARPO_I", "PARPO I", "PARPO1", "PARPO_1"].includes(r)) && ['PENDING_PARPO', 'APPROVED', 'READY_FOR_PRINT'].includes(o.status)) canSeeApproved = true; if (roles.some(r => ["PARPO", "VEHICLE_APPROVER", "VEHICLE APPROVER", "ADMIN", "HRMO"].includes(r)) && ['APPROVED', 'READY_FOR_PRINT'].includes(o.status)) canSeeApproved = true; if (canSeeApproved) { show = true; if (o.status === 'PENDING_CHIEF') { displayStatus = 'PENDING CHIEF'; borderColor = 'border-yellow-500'; textColor = 'text-yellow-600'; // ADDED: Styling for PENDING PARPO I } else if (o.status === 'PENDING_PARPO_I') { displayStatus = 'PENDING PARPO I'; borderColor = 'border-purple-500'; textColor = 'text-purple-600'; } else if (o.status === 'PENDING_PARPO') { displayStatus = 'PENDING PARPO II'; borderColor = 'border-blue-500'; textColor = 'text-blue-600'; } else if (o.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' || o.status === 'APPROVED') { displayStatus = o.status === 'READY_FOR_PRINT' ? } } } combinedRecords.sort((a,b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at)); if (combinedRecords.length === 0) { content.innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full opacity-50"><span class="text-4xl">📄</span><p class="font-bold mt-2 text-xs uppercase">No Records</p></div>`; } else { let h = `<div class="p-4 space-y-3 pb-32">`; const toRender = combinedRecords.slice(0, maxLimit); toRender.forEach(r => { h += r.html; }); content.innerHTML = h + `</div>`; } } // --- REPORTS (WHEREABOUTS) --- async function loadReportsTab() { const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); }); container.innerHTML = h; } // --- SCOPED ANALYTICS TAB --- async function loadAnalyticsTab() { const content = document.getElementById('mainContent'); content.innerHTML = `<p class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-slate-500 font-bold animate-pulse">Compiling Scoped Overview...</p>`; window.switchLeaveSubTab = function(subTab) { currentLeaveSubTab = subTab; selectedLeaves.clear(); updateBatchActionBar(); const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('header + div button'); tabs.forEach(t => { t.classList.remove('border-emerald-700', 'text-emerald-800'); t.classList.add('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); }); const tabMap = { 'pending': 0, 'current': 1, 'incoming': 2, 'previous': 3, 'calendar': 4 }; const activeIdx = tabMap[subTab]; if(tabs[activeIdx]) { tabs[activeIdx].classList.remove('border-transparent', 'text-slate-500'); tabs[activeIdx].classList.add('border-emerald-700', 'text-emerald-800'); } if (subTab === 'calendar') { loadAdminCalendar(); } else { loadLeavesTab(); } }; const checkScope = (div, unit) => { const roles = currentUser.roles || []; if (roles.some(r => ["PARPO", "ADMIN", "HRMO"].includes(r))) return true; const oDivs = div ? div.split(',').map(x => x.trim().toUpperCase()) : []; const dMatch = oDivs.some(d => currentUser.divisions.includes(d)); if (roles.includes("CHIEF")) return dMatch; const oUnits = unit ? unit.split(',').map(x => x.trim().toUpperCase()) : []; const uMatch = oUnits.some(u => currentUser.units.includes(u)); return dMatch && uMatch; }; function getLeaveStatus(remarks) { const m = remarks.match(/ROUTING_STATUS:\s*([^\s|]+)/); return m ? m[1].toUpperCase() : 'PENDING_CHIEF'; } function setLeaveStatus(remarks, newStatus) { if (remarks.includes('ROUTING_STATUS:')) return remarks.replace(/ROUTING_STATUS:\s*([^\s|]+)/, `ROUTING_STATUS: ${newStatus}`); return remarks + ` | ROUTING_STATUS: ${newStatus}`; } // CTO FEFO (First-Expiring, First-Out): chronological consumption by expiry, // NOT by credit application/posting date. Old deductions without an allocation // are reconstructed in FEFO order. New deductions pin their source IDs in remarks. const CTOFEFO = (() => { const EPS = 0.000001; const onlyDate = value => { const match = String(value ?? '').match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/); return match ? match[1] : ''; }; const today = () => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {timeZone:'Asia/Manila',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date()); const addYear = date => { if (!date) return ''; const d = new Date(date + 'T00:00:00Z'); if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; d.setUTCFullYear(d.getUTCFullYear()+1); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); }; const amount = row => Math.max(0, Math.abs(Number(row.calculated_hours ?? row.base_hours ?? 0) || 0)); const orderRef = row => { const value = String(row.PSO_Number ?? row.pso_number ?? '').trim(); return value ? (/^(PSO|RSO|SO)\b/i.test(value) ? value : 'PSO ' + value) : 'CTO credit #' + String(row.id ?? '?'); }; const isEarned = row => { const t=String(row.transaction_type||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const r=String(row.remarks||'').trim().toUpperCase(); return t==='EARNED' && !['APPLICATION','PENDING','REQUESTED','SUBMITTED','DISAPPROVED','REJECTED','CANCELLED'].includes(t) && !['FOR HR PROCESSING','ENCODED','PENDING','APPLICATION','FOR PROCESSING'].includes(r) && !/^(DISAPPROVED|REJECTED|CANCELLED)/.test(r); }; const isUsed = row => { const t=String(row.transaction_type||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const r=String(row.remarks||'').trim().toUpperCase(); return t==='USED'||t==='CTO DEDUCTION'||r==='CTO DEDUCTION'||r.startsWith('CTO DEDUCTION |'); }; const expiry = row => onlyDate(row.expires_on) || addYear(onlyDate(row.pso_date||row.date||row.created_at)); const earnedDate = row => onlyDate(row.pso_date||row.date||row.created_at||row.posted_at); const useDate = row => onlyDate(row.leave_date||row.date||row.posted_at||row.created_at); const compareBuckets = (a,b) => (a.expiresOn||'9999-12-31').localeCompare(b.expiresOn||'9999-12-31') || a.earnedDate.localeCompare(b.earnedDate) || String(a.record.id??'').localeCompare(String(b.record.id??''),undefined,{numeric:true}); // Each stored new USED transaction has an audit tag, e.g. FEFO:72=3.000,76=5.000. // BASE is used only for the pre-system opening balance without an expiry date. const readPinned = remarks => { const m=String(remarks||'').match(/(?:^|\s|\|)FEFO:([A-Za-z0-9_=.\-,]+)/); if(!m) return null; return m[1].split(',').map(part=>{ const i=part.lastIndexOf('='); return i<1?null:{id:part.slice(0,i),hours:Math.max(0,Number(part.slice(i+1))||0)}; }).filter(Boolean); }; const snapshot = (employee, allRecords, asOf=today()) => { const cutoff=onlyDate(asOf)||today(); const empId=String(employee?.emp_id ?? ''); const records=(allRecords||[]).filter(r=>!empId || String(r.emp_id)===empId); const buckets=records.filter(isEarned).map(record=>({ record, id:String(record.id ?? ''), ref:orderRef(record), earnedDate:earnedDate(record), expiresOn:expiry(record), originalHours:amount(record), remainingHours:amount(record) })).filter(b=>b.earnedDate && b.earnedDate<=cutoff).sort(compareBuckets); let openingRemaining=Math.max(0,Number(employee?.cto_balance||0)||0); let unmatchedHours=0; const uses=records.filter(isUsed).filter(r=>{const d=useDate(r);return !!d && d<=cutoff;}) .sort((a,b)=>useDate(a).localeCompare(useDate(b)) || Number(a.id||0)-Number(b.id||0)); for(const use of uses){ const date=useDate(use); let required=amount(use); const pinned=readPinned(use.remarks); if(pinned){ for(const item of pinned){ if(required<=EPS)break; const qty=Math.min(required,item.hours); if(item.id==='BASE'){ const take=Math.min(openingRemaining,qty);openingRemaining-=take;unmatchedHours+=qty-take; }else{ const bucket=buckets.find(b=>b.id===item.id); const take=bucket?Math.min(bucket.remainingHours,qty):0; if(bucket)bucket.remainingHours-=take; unmatchedHours+=qty-take; } required-=qty; } // Any unexpected unmatched amount is not silently assigned to another PSO. unmatchedHours+=Math.max(0,required); }else{ for(const bucket of buckets){ if(required<=EPS)break; if(bucket.earnedDate>date || (bucket.expiresOn && date>=bucket.expiresOn))continue; const take=Math.min(required,bucket.remainingHours); bucket.remainingHours-=take;required-=take; } if(required>EPS){const take=Math.min(required,openingRemaining);openingRemaining-=take;required-=take;} unmatchedHours+=Math.max(0,required); } } const active=buckets.filter(b=>b.earnedDate<=cutoff && (!b.expiresOn||cutoff<b.expiresOn) && b.remainingHours>EPS); const availableHours=openingRemaining+active.reduce((sum,b)=>sum+b.remainingHours,0); const expiredHours=buckets.filter(b=>b.expiresOn && cutoff>=b.expiresOn) .reduce((sum,b)=>sum+b.remainingHours,0); const plan=(requested, forDate=cutoff) => { let needed=Math.max(0,Number(requested)||0); const selections=[]; for(const b of active){ if(needed<=EPS)break; if(b.earnedDate>forDate || (b.expiresOn&&forDate>=b.expiresOn))continue; const hrs=Math.min(needed,b.remainingHours); if(hrs>EPS){selections.push({id:b.id,ref:b.ref,hours:hrs,expiresOn:b.expiresOn});needed-=hrs;} } if(needed>EPS && openingRemaining>EPS){const hrs=Math.min(needed,openingRemaining); selections.push({id:'BASE',ref:'Opening CTO balance',hours:hrs,expiresOn:''});needed-=hrs;} return {selections,unfundedHours:Math.max(0,needed),hoursUsed:Math.max(0,Number(requested)||0)-Math.max(0,needed)}; }; return {availableHours,openingRemaining,expiredHours,unmatchedHours,buckets,active,nextToExpire:active[0]||null,plan}; }; const tag = plan => 'FEFO:'+plan.selections.map(b=>`${b.id}=${b.hours.toFixed(3)}`).join(','); const label = plan => plan.selections.map(b=>`${b.ref}: ${b.hours.toFixed(3)}h${b.expiresOn?' (expires '+b.expiresOn+')':''}`).join('; '); return {snapshot,today,expiry,earnedDate,orderRef,isEarned,isUsed,tag,label,onlyDate,addYear}; })(); function getLeaveAppPayload(u, leaveRec) { const roles = u.roles || []; const currentStatus = getLeaveStatus(leaveRec.remarks || ""); let nextStatus = currentStatus; let transType = "APPLICATION"; if (currentStatus === "PENDING_UNIT_HEAD" && roles.some(r => ["UNIT_HEAD", "UNIT HEAD", "MARPO"].includes(r))) { nextStatus = "PENDING_CHIEF"; } else if (currentStatus === "PENDING_CHIEF" && roles.includes("CHIEF")) { nextStatus = "PENDING_PARPO"; } else if (currentStatus === "PENDING_PARPO" && roles.includes("PARPO")) { nextStatus = "PENDING_HARDCOPY"; } else if (currentStatus === "PENDING_HARDCOPY" || currentStatus === "PENDING_HRMO") { // Final approval and hard-copy validation are ONLY available on HR Leave Processing. return null; } if (nextStatus === currentStatus) return null; return { transaction_type: transType, remarks: setLeaveStatus(leaveRec.remarks || "", nextStatus) }; } async function postCtoDeductionIfFinalApproval(leaveRec, payload) { if (!leaveRec || !payload || payload.transaction_type !== 'APPROVED') return; const remarksUpper = String(leaveRec.remarks || '').toUpperCase(); const isCtoLeave = remarksUpper.includes('CREDIT TO CTO') || remarksUpper.includes('CREDITED TO CTO'); if (!isCtoLeave) return; const ctoHoursMatch = String(leaveRec.remarks || '').match(/CTO HOURS:\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/i); const hoursToUse = ctoHoursMatch ? (Math.abs(parseFloat(ctoHoursMatch[1])) || 0) : ((Math.abs(parseFloat(leaveRec.debit || 0)) || 0) * 8); // backward compatibility for older applications if (hoursToUse <= 0) throw new Error("Invalid CTO hours for this leave."); const purposeKey = `CREDIT TO CTO | LEAVE_ID:${leaveRec.id}`; const { data: existing, error: existingErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .select('id') .eq('emp_id', leaveRec.emp_id) .eq('purpose', purposeKey) .limit(1); if (existingErr) throw existingErr; if (existing && existing.length > 0) return; const { data: empRow, error: empErr } = await supabaseClient .from('employees') .select('cto_balance') .eq('emp_id', leaveRec.emp_id) .maybeSingle(); if (empErr) throw empErr; let availableHours = (parseFloat(empRow?.cto_balance || 0) || 0); const { data: ctoRows, error: ctoErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .select('transaction_type, remarks, calculated_hours') .eq('emp_id', leaveRec.emp_id); if (ctoErr) throw ctoErr; (ctoRows || []).forEach(r => { const ttype = String(r.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const rmk = String(r.remarks || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const hrs = Math.abs(parseFloat(String(r.calculated_hours || '0').trim()) || 0); if (window.isCtoCreditedEarnedRecord(r)) { availableHours += hrs; } else if (window.isCtoUsedRecord(r)) { availableHours -= hrs; } }); const { data: pendingRows, error: pendingErr } = await supabaseClient .from('leave_ledger') .select('id, debit, remarks') .eq('emp_id', leaveRec.emp_id) .eq('transaction_type', 'APPLICATION'); if (pendingErr) throw pendingErr; (pendingRows || []).forEach(l => { if (String(l.id) === String(leaveRec.id)) return; const lr = String(l.remarks || '').toUpperCase(); if (lr.includes('CREDIT TO CTO') || lr.includes('CREDITED TO CTO')) { const pendingHoursMatch = String(l.remarks || '').match(/CTO HOURS:\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/i); const pendingHours = pendingHoursMatch ? (Math.abs(parseFloat(pendingHoursMatch[1])) || 0) : ((Math.abs(parseFloat(l.debit || 0)) || 0) * 8); availableHours -= pendingHours; } }); if (availableHours + 0.0001 < hoursToUse) { throw new Error(`Insufficient CTO balance. Available: ${availableHours.toFixed(2)} hour(s); required: ${hoursToUse.toFixed(2)} hour(s).`); } const todayPST = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Manila' }).format(new Date()); const { error: insertErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .insert({ emp_id: leaveRec.emp_id, date: todayPST, transaction_type: 'USED', base_type: 'CREDIT TO CTO', base_hours: hoursToUse.toFixed(2), multiplier: '1.0', calculated_hours: hoursToUse.toFixed(2), remarks: 'CTO DEDUCTION', purpose: purposeKey, leave_date: leaveRec.start_date }); if (insertErr) throw insertErr; } async function applyLeaveApprovalUpdate(leaveData, payload) { const original = { transaction_type: leaveData.transaction_type, remarks: leaveData.remarks }; const { error: updateErr } = await supabaseClient .from('leave_ledger') .update(payload) .eq('id', leaveData.id); } } function getStatusBadge(type, subStatus) { if (type === 'APPLICATION') return `<span class="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 animate-pulse"></span>${subStatus || 'PENDING'}</span>`; 'text-emerald-800' : 'text-amber-800'; const safeObj = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(l)); let actionBtn = ''; if (isFutureOrToday) { actionBtn = `<button onclick="openRetractModal('${safeObj}')" class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border shadow-sm rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors w-full sm:w-auto">⚙️ Manage</button>`; } dayLeavesHtml += ` <div class="${bgClass} border rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-sm"> <div class="flex items-center gap-3"> <div class="shrink-0 text-xl">${isApproved ? const [lRes, empRes] = await Promise.all([ supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').select('*').in('transaction_type', ['APPLICATION', 'APPROVED', 'DISAPPROVED', 'CANCELLED', 'RETRACTED']).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3000), supabaseClient.from('employees').select('emp_id, full_name, division, unit') ]);
