if (['DISAPPROVED', 'CANCELLED', 'RETRACTED'].includes(l.transaction_type) || l.transaction_type.includes('DISAPPROVED')) show = true; } if (show) { let btns = ''; if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'pending') { const denyControl = mayDisapproveLeave(l.start_date) ? `<button onclick="openDenyModal('${l.id}')" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-rose-200 text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest shadow-sm transition-colors uppercase w-full sm:w-auto">Deny</button>` : `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700">Disapproval closed (deadline: ${leaveDisapprovalCutoff(l.start_date) || 'missing date'})</span>`; btns = `${denyControl} <button onclick="processDirectLeave('${l.id}', 'APPROVE')" class="px-5 py-2.5 dar-green hover:bg-emerald-900 text-white rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest shadow-sm transition-colors uppercase w-full sm:w-auto mt-2 sm:mt-0">Approve</button>`; } else if (l.transaction_type === 'APPROVED' || l.transaction_type === 'APPLICATION') { // Pass stringified JSON object securely const safeObj = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(l)); btns = `<button onclick="openRetractModal('${safeObj}')" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-indigo-200 text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest shadow-sm transition-colors uppercase w-full sm:w-auto">⚙️ Retract Dates</button>`; } const badge = getStatusBadge(l.transaction_type, s); const isPending = currentLeaveSubTab === 'pending'; itemsToRender.push({ emp: emp, leaveData: l, html: ` <div class="p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start gap-4 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors relative"> ${isPending && mayDisapproveLeave(l.start_date) ? `<div class="shrink-0 mt-1"><input type="checkbox" value="${l.id}" class="batch-checkbox" onchange="toggleBatchSelection('${l.id}', this.checked)"></div>` : ''} <div class="flex-grow w-full"> <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-2"> <span class="font-black text-slate-800 text-sm tracking-tight">${emp.full_name}</span> ${badge} </div> <div class="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-1.5 leading-tight flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-1 sm:gap-2"> <span class="font-bold text-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-100">${l.type}</span> <span class="font-black text-slate-700 bg-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${l.debit} Days</span> <span class="text-slate-500 font-semibold bg-white border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-sm">📅 ${l.start_date} to ${l.end_date}</span> </div> <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-600 bg-white px-3 py-2 rounded-xl mt-3 border border-slate-200 leading-relaxed break-words whitespace-normal shadow-inner"> <span class="font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-[9px] block mb-0.5">Remarks / Logistics</span> ${l.remarks || 'No additional remarks provided'} </div> </div> <div class="shrink-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto mt-2 sm:mt-0 pt-2 sm:pt-0 border-t sm:border-t-0 border-slate-100"> ${btns} </div> </div>` }); } } } // Sort items logically based on view if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'incoming') { itemsToRender.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.leaveData.start_date) - new Date(b.leaveData.start_date)); } let h = `<div class="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">`; // KPI Dashboard Summary if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'pending') { h += ` <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-2"> <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-center items-start"> <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Queue Actionable</span> <span class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-700 leading-none">${totalQueue}</span> </div> <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-center items-start"> <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Pending Application</span> <span class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-700 leading-none">${itemsToRender.length}</span> <!-- Informative proxy --> </div> </div> `; } else if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'current') { h += ` <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 mb-2"> <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-center items-start"> <span class="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">On Leave Today</span> <span class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-700 leading-none">${itemsToRender.length}</span> </div> </div> `; } if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'pending' && itemsToRender.length > 0) { h += ` <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-white px-5 py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm"> <label class="flex items-center space-x-3 cursor-pointer text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 group"> <input type="checkbox" class="batch-checkbox" onchange="selectAllPending(this)"> <span class="group-hover:text-emerald-700 transition-colors uppercase tracking-widest text-xs">Select All Pending</span> </label> <span class="text-[10px] font-black bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-widest shadow-inner">${itemsToRender.length} Leaves</span> </div> `; } if (itemsToRender.length === 0) { h += `<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-24 opacity-40"><span class="text-6xl mb-4">🍃</span><p class="font-black text-sm tracking-widest uppercase text-slate-500">No Records Found</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2 text-center max-w-xs">You are completely caught up in this section.</p></div>`; } else { const grouped = {}; itemsToRender.forEach(item => { const div = item.emp.division || "UNASSIGNED DIVISION"; const unit = item.emp.unit || "UNASSIGNED UNIT"; if (!grouped[div]) grouped[div] = {}; if (!grouped[div][unit]) grouped[div][unit] = []; grouped[div][unit].push(item); }); for (const [div, units] of Object.entries(grouped)) { h += ` <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden mb-6 last:mb-0"> <div class="bg-slate-800 text-white px-5 py-3 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2"> <span>🏢</span> ${div} </div> <div class="p-2 bg-slate-50">`; for (const [unit, items] of Object.entries(units)) { h += ` <div class="mb-2 last:mb-0 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"> <h4 class="text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-800 uppercase bg-emerald-50/50 px-5 py-2.5 border-b border-emerald-100/50 flex items-center gap-2"> <span class="text-emerald-500">↳</span> ${unit} </h4> <div class="divide-y divide-slate-100">`; items.forEach(i => h += i.html); h += ` </div> </div>`; } h += `</div></div>`; } } content.innerHTML = h + `</div>`; } // MODAL LOGIC FOR DISAPPROVAL AND RETRACTION window.closeModals = function() { document.getElementById('denyModal').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('retractModal').style.display = 'none'; currentActionContext = null; } // Strict leave disapproval cutoff: until 11:59 PM Asia/Manila on the // calendar day BEFORE the first scheduled leave day. No same-day or // retroactive denial, regardless of role or whether HR already approved. function leaveDisapprovalTodayPHT() { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Manila', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); } function leaveDisapprovalCutoff(startDate) { const date = String(startDate || '').slice(0, 10); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''; const parsed = new Date(date + 'T00:00:00Z'); if (!Number.isFinite(parsed.getTime()) || parsed.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== date) return ''; parsed.setUTCDate(parsed.getUTCDate() - 1); return parsed.toISOString().slice(0, 10); } function mayDisapproveLeave(startDate) { const cutoff = leaveDisapprovalCutoff(startDate); return Boolean(cutoff && leaveDisapprovalTodayPHT() <= cutoff); } function requireDisapprovalBeforeLeave(startDate) { const cutoff = leaveDisapprovalCutoff(startDate); if (!cutoff) throw new Error('The leave start date is missing or invalid; disapproval cannot proceed.'); if (!mayDisapproveLeave(startDate)) { throw new Error('Disapproval deadline passed. This leave began on ' + String(startDate).slice(0,10) + '. The last permitted disapproval date was ' + cutoff + ' (Philippine time). Same-day and retroactive disapproval are not allowed.'); } } window.openDenyModal = async function(id) { try { const selectedIds = id === 'BATCH' ? Array.from(selectedLeaves) : [id]; if (!selectedIds.length) return; const {data, error} = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger') .select('id, start_date, transaction_type').in('id', selectedIds); if (error) throw error; if (!data || data.length !== selectedIds.length) throw new Error('One or more applications could not be found. Refresh the leave list.'); for (const item of data) { if (String(item.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase() !== 'APPLICATION') throw new Error('Only pending applications may be disapproved at this approval level.'); requireDisapprovalBeforeLeave(item.start_date); } currentActionContext = id; document.getElementById('denyReasonInput').value = ''; document.getElementById('denyModal').style.display = 'flex'; } catch (e) { alert('Disapproval unavailable: ' + e.message); } } window.confirmDeny = async function() { const reason = document.getElementById('denyReasonInput').value.trim(); if (!reason) return alert("A mandatory reason for disapproval is required."); // Revalidate against the latest database record on submission, not just // when the modal opened. This also protects batches containing old cases. const denialContext = currentActionContext; const targetIds = denialContext === 'BATCH' ? Array.from(selectedLeaves) : [denialContext]; if (!targetIds.length || !targetIds[0]) return alert('Please select an application.'); const {data: freshLeaves, error: freshError} = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger') .select('id, start_date, transaction_type').in('id', targetIds); if (freshError) return alert('Cannot verify disapproval deadline: ' + freshError.message); if (!freshLeaves || freshLeaves.length !== targetIds.length) return alert('A selected application is missing. Refresh the page.'); try { for (const rec of freshLeaves) { if (String(rec.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase() !== 'APPLICATION') throw new Error('A selected application is no longer pending.'); requireDisapprovalBeforeLeave(rec.start_date); } } catch (e) { return alert('Disapproval unavailable: ' + e.message); } closeModals(); if (denialContext === 'BATCH') { const leaveIds = Array.from(selectedLeaves); document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col h-[70vh] justify-center items-center"><span class="text-4xl mb-4 animate-spin block">⏳</span><p class="font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-rose-600">Processing Batch Denial...</p></div>`; const { data: lResData } = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').select('*').in('id', leaveIds); const leavePromises = leaveIds.map(async (id) => { const leaveData = (lResData || []).find(l => l.id === id); if (leaveData) { const priorApprovalStage = getLeaveStatus(leaveData.remarks || ''); const newRemarks = setLeaveStatus(leaveData.remarks || "", 'DISAPPROVED') + ` | DENIED_AT: ${priorApprovalStage} | Disapproved: ${String(reason).replace(/\|/g,' / ')} | Denied by ${currentUser.fullName}`; await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').update({ transaction_type: 'DISAPPROVED', remarks: newRemarks }).eq('id', id); } }); await Promise.all(leavePromises); selectedLeaves.clear(); updateBatchActionBar(); } else { document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col h-[70vh] justify-center items-center"><span class="text-4xl mb-4 animate-spin block">⏳</span><p class="font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-rose-600">Denying Record...</p></div>`; const { data: leaveData } = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').select('*').eq('id', denialContext).single(); if (leaveData) { const priorApprovalStage = getLeaveStatus(leaveData.remarks || ''); const newRemarks = setLeaveStatus(leaveData.remarks || "", 'DISAPPROVED') + ` | DENIED_AT: ${priorApprovalStage} | Disapproved: ${String(reason).replace(/\|/g,' / ')} | Denied by ${currentUser.fullName}`; await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').update({ transaction_type: 'DISAPPROVED', remarks: newRemarks }).eq('id', denialContext); } } loadLeavesTab(); } window.openRetractModal = function(safeObjEncoded) { const leaveObj = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(safeObjEncoded)); currentActionContext = leaveObj; const start = new Date(leaveObj.start_date + "T00:00:00"); const end = new Date(leaveObj.end_date + "T00:00:00"); let datesHtml = ''; let curr = new Date(start); while (curr <= end) { let day = curr.getDay(); let isWeekend = (day === 0 || day === 6); let dateStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0]; let displayStr = curr.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); // Highlight weekends so admin is aware datesHtml += ` <label class="flex items-center space-x-3 p-3 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl ${isWeekend ? 'bg-slate-100 opacity-60' : 'bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50/50'} cursor-pointer transition-colors shadow-sm"> <input type="checkbox" value="${dateStr}" class="retract-date-cb w-5 h-5 rounded border-slate-300 text-indigo-600 focus:ring-indigo-500" ${isWeekend ? '' : 'checked'}> <span class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">${displayStr} ${isWeekend ? '<span class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 ml-2 font-black">(Weekend)</span>' : ''}</span> </label> `; curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1); } document.getElementById('retractDatesContainer').innerHTML = datesHtml; document.getElementById('retractReasonInput').value = ''; document.getElementById('retractModal').style.display = 'flex'; } window.confirmRetract = async function() { const leaveData = currentActionContext; const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.retract-date-cb:checked'); const reason = document.getElementById('retractReasonInput').value.trim(); if (checkboxes.length === 0) return alert("Please select at least one specific date to retract."); if (!reason) return alert("A mandatory reason for retraction is required to ensure audit compliance."); const retractedDates = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value); let daysToRetract = checkboxes.length; // Failsafe: Never retract more than the logged debit if (daysToRetract > leaveData.debit) { daysToRetract = leaveData.debit; } closeModals(); document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col h-[70vh] justify-center items-center"><span class="text-4xl mb-4 animate-spin block">⚙️</span><p class="font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-600">Processing Retraction Ledger Protocol...</p></div>`; try { const todayStr = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Manila' }).format(new Date()); // 1. Refund the same credit source that funded the leave. const leaveRemarksUpper = String(leaveData.remarks || '').toUpperCase(); const isCtoLeave = leaveRemarksUpper.includes('CREDIT TO CTO') || leaveRemarksUpper.includes('CREDITED TO CTO'); if (isCtoLeave) { const purposeKey = `CREDIT TO CTO | LEAVE_ID:${leaveData.id}`; const { data: ctoDeduction, error: ctoFetchErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .select('id, calculated_hours, base_hours') .eq('emp_id', leaveData.emp_id) .eq('purpose', purposeKey) .maybeSingle(); if (ctoFetchErr) throw ctoFetchErr; if (ctoDeduction) { const refundHours = daysToRetract * 8; const currentHours = Math.abs(parseFloat(ctoDeduction.calculated_hours || 0)) || 0; const remainingHours = Math.max(0, currentHours - refundHours); if (remainingHours <= 0.0001) { const { error: delErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .delete() .eq('id', ctoDeduction.id); if (delErr) throw delErr; } else { const { error: updErr } = await supabaseClient .from('cto_records') .update({ calculated_hours: remainingHours.toFixed(2), base_hours: remainingHours.toFixed(2) }) .eq('id', ctoDeduction.id); if (updErr) throw updErr; } } } else { await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').insert({ transaction_id: crypto.randomUUID(), emp_id: leaveData.emp_id, start_date: todayStr, end_date: todayStr, type: leaveData.type, credit: daysToRetract, transaction_type: "CREDIT", remarks: `System Refund: Retracted ${daysToRetract} day(s) on ${retractedDates.join(', ')}. Processed by: ${currentUser.fullName}` }); } // 2. Update the existing transaction record specifically mapped to RETRACTED or reduced partial days if (daysToRetract >= leaveData.debit) { // Fully Retracted implies the whole chunk goes back await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').update({ transaction_type: 'RETRACTED', remarks: (leaveData.remarks || '') + ` | FULLY RETRACTED BY ${currentUser.fullName}: ${reason}` }).eq('id', leaveData.id); } else { // Partially Retracted (Deduct the days from original leave but leave status as APPROVED) await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').update({ debit: leaveData.debit - daysToRetract, remarks: (leaveData.remarks || '') + ` | PARTIALLY RETRACTED DATES: [${retractedDates.join(', ')}] BY ${currentUser.fullName}: ${reason}` }).eq('id', leaveData.id); } alert(`Successfully retracted ${daysToRetract} day(s) and refunded the employee's credits.`); } catch(e) { alert("Database Error: " + e.message); } // Reload whichever tab we were currently looking at if (currentLeaveSubTab === 'calendar') { loadAdminCalendar(); } else { loadLeavesTab(); } } // BATCH & TOGGLE SELECTIONS window.toggleBatchSelection = function(id, isChecked) { if (isChecked) selectedLeaves.add(id); else selectedLeaves.delete(id); updateBatchActionBar(); } window.selectAllPending = function(checkbox) { const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.batch-checkbox'); checkboxes.forEach(cb => { if(cb.value && cb.value !== "on") { cb.checked = checkbox.checked; if (checkbox.checked) selectedLeaves.add(cb.value); else selectedLeaves.delete(cb.value); } }); updateBatchActionBar(); } function updateBatchActionBar() { const bar = document.getElementById('batchActionBar'); if (selectedLeaves.size > 0) { document.getElementById('batchCountText').innerText = `${selectedLeaves.size} selected`; bar.classList.remove('translate-y-full'); } else bar.classList.add('translate-y-full'); } window.processBatchLeave = async function(action) { if (selectedLeaves.size === 0) return; if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { openDenyModal('BATCH'); return; } updateBatchActionBar(); document.getElementById('batchActionBar').classList.add('translate-y-full'); const leaveIds = Array.from(selectedLeaves); document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col h-[70vh] justify-center items-center"><span class="text-4xl mb-4 animate-spin block">⏳</span><p class="font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-700">Approving Batch Leaves...</p></div>`; const { data: lResData } = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').select('*').in('id', leaveIds); const leavePromises = leaveIds.map(async (id) => { const leaveData = (lResData || []).find(l => l.id === id); if (leaveData) { const p = getLeaveAppPayload(currentUser, leaveData); if (p && Object.keys(p).length > 0) { await applyLeaveApprovalUpdate(leaveData, p); } } }); await Promise.all(leavePromises); selectedLeaves.clear(); loadLeavesTab(); }; window.processDirectLeave = async function(id, action) { if (action === 'DISAPPROVED') { openDenyModal(id); return; } const { data: leaveData } = await supabaseClient.from('leave_ledger').select('*').eq('id', id).single(); if (!leaveData) return; const p = getLeaveAppPayload(currentUser, leaveData); if (!p || Object.keys(p).length === 0) return alert("Approval failed: Your role is not configured to approve this record."); document.getElementById('mainContent').innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col h-[70vh] justify-center items-center"><span class="text-4xl mb-4 animate-spin block">⏳</span><p class="font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-700">Advancing Leave Approval...</p></div>`; try { await applyLeaveApprovalUpdate(leaveData, p); } catch (e) { alert("Approval Error: " + e.message); } loadLeavesTab(); }; checkSession(); render(); </script> </body> </html>
